# [2] About Treel — Building the Mobility Intelligence Category | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/about  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/about  
**Meta Description:** *Treel Mobility Solutions is India&#x27;s mobility intelligence company. Founded 2018 by Manish Ingale. Backed by JK Tyre. Headquartered in Pune. 200+ enterprise customers, 68K vehicles under management.*  

### Page Content

THE COMPANY

# We started with a tyre. We are building a category.

Treel is the mobility intelligence company. What began in 2018 as a tyre pressure sensor is now the Vehicle Digital Twin architecture that operates across India&#x27;s largest connected commercial vehicle dataset.

## Why Treel exists.

In 2018, India had four million commercial vehicles on the road, but fleet operators had no clarity into real-time vehicle telemetry. Fleet owners knew what they had bought. They knew what they had paid for fuel. They knew when a truck broke down. Between those three data points, they had no clarity on daily operating conditions.

Treel was founded to resolve that lack of clarity. We started at the tyre because it is the vehicle&#x27;s most talkative component, and because JK Tyre had spent five decades understanding what tyres try to tell you. The tyre was the entry point. The vehicle was always the destination.

Today, our core purpose is framed around 360-degree vehicle visibility and emotional safety. By transforming commercial vehicles into queryable intelligence surfaces, we replace operational fear and highway vulnerability with absolute certainty—giving drivers peace of mind in the cabin, fleet managers predictable control, and families the emotional reassurance of a safe return.

## One architecture. Three audiences.

Today Treel operates three product families on one shared data infrastructure. TMIP serves enterprise fleets managing ten or more vehicles. Project Suraksha serves commercial vehicle owner-drivers, one truck at a time, across India&#x27;s key highway freight corridors. All three share the Vehicle Digital Twin architecture, the sensor family, and the operational DNA that binds Treel together.

[ ENTERPRISE FLEETS

### TMIP Enterprise

Vehicle Digital Twin platform for fleets with 10 to 10,000+ commercial vehicles.

Explore TMIP ](/tmip) [ OWNER-DRIVERS

### Project Suraksha

Puncture avoidance, tyre life extension, and 15-min driver helpline across Indian highways.

Explore Suraksha ](/suraksha) [ CONSUMERS

### Personal TPMS

Smart Bluetooth tyre pressure monitoring kits. Fits any car or two-wheeler.

Explore Personal ](/personal)

## What we believe.

### Data before opinion.

Every claim we make has a measurement behind it. Fleet operators do not need another vendor with a pitch. They need numbers that hold up in a QBR. We build for that room.

### Simplicity is a feature.

A truck driver installing Suraksha at a roadside puncture shop should not need to think about our engineering. A CFO reviewing TMIP&#x27;s ROI should not need to interpret a dashboard. Simplicity is not a design polish; it is the product working correctly.

### Category-defining, quietly.

We are moving an industry from monitoring components to reasoning about vehicles. We do this by shipping, not by declaring. The category will settle on the definition of the company that operates it.

### Indian first. Global ready.

We built Treel for Indian roads, Indian fleets, and Indian owner-drivers. The architecture ports cleanly to Middle Eastern and European markets from Month 4 onward, without compromising the Indian core.

## The people behind Treel.

Treel is led by founder Manish Ingale, with a leadership team drawn from tyre engineering, enterprise software, and fleet operations.

[Meet the full leadership team ](/leadership) MANUFACTURING & FIELD OPERATIONS

## A JK Tyre technology company.

Treel is JK Tyre&#x27;s technology arm for mobility intelligence. JK Tyre backs Treel with five decades of tyre engineering, national distribution reach, and the operational credibility that comes with one of India&#x27;s most trusted commercial tyre brands. Treel operates independently on product, platform, and go-to-market decisions.

# [3] Accessibility · Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/accessibility  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/accessibility  
**Meta Description:** *Treel&#x27;s commitment to accessibility. Our WCAG 2.2 Level AA target, current status, and how to report accessibility issues.*  

### Page Content

LEGAL

# Accessibility Statement

Effective date: 1 October 2026 Last updated: 1 October 2026 Target: WCAG 2.2 Level AA treel.in/accessibility Table of Contents Accessibility Support [ hello@treel.in](mailto:hello@treel.in?subject=Accessibility%20feedback) [ 1800 833 0233 (Toll-Free)](tel:18008330233)

## 1. Our commitment

Treel is committed to ensuring that treel.in and our digital products are accessible to people with disabilities. We believe technology should be usable by everyone, regardless of ability.

From our web presence to vehicle telematics interfaces, our digital design and engineering systems actively integrate universal design principles so that fleet operators, drivers, enterprise managers, and everyday vehicle owners can interact with our information seamlessly.

## 2. Standards we aim for

We aim to conform to the Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) 2.2 at Level AA . These guidelines are the international standard for web accessibility.

WCAG 2.2

### International Benchmark

Covers comprehensive digital accessibility principles: Perceivable, Operable, Understandable, and Robust (POUR).

Level AA

### Conformance Target

Requires rigorous contrast ratios, assistive screen-reader interoperability, full keyboard navigation, and cognitive clarity.

## 3. Current accessibility features

Our website includes the following accessibility features:

### Semantic HTML Structure

Structured layout with hierarchical headings (H1–H4), landmarks, and semantic HTML elements.

### Descriptive Alt Text

Descriptive alt text provided for all meaningful photographs, hardware diagrams, and system icons.

### Keyboard Navigation

Comprehensive keyboard accessibility across all interactive buttons, links, menus, and controls.

### Visible Focus Indicators

High-contrast, prominent focus indicators ensuring clear visual tracking across all interactive states.

### Compliant Colour Contrast

Ratios strictly meeting WCAG AA standards (minimum 4.5:1 for body copy and 3:1 for large display text).

### Skip-to-Content Links

Direct bypass skip links allowing keyboard and screen-reader users to skip repetitive navigation.

### Screen Reader Support

Compatible and verified with industry-standard assistive readers including VoiceOver, NVDA, and JAWS.

### Text Alternatives

Equivalent textual alternatives and labels for data visualisations and non-text interactive components.

### Responsive Design

Fluid page scaling across mobile, tablet, desktop, and system magnification settings up to 200%.

### Video Captions and Transcripts

Captions and transcripts provided for explanatory product and tutorial videos where applicable.

## 4. Known limitations

We are working on improving:

0 1 . Complete keyboard navigation on all form components In Progress

Optimization is currently in active progress for the multi-step Suraksha EMI application form to ensure seamless focus-trap management and error announcements.

0 2 . Language attribute markup for Hinglish content In Progress

Fine-tuning xml:lang and lang attribute segmentation across vernacular and Hinglish educational sections on Suraksha driver-facing pages.

0 3 . Screen reader compatibility with interactive Vehicle Digital Twin Alternative View Available

The three-dimensional interactive telemetry canvas on TMIP is being enriched with granular ARIA live regions; an alternative accessible data table is provided as a direct fallback.

[ Visit TMIP ](/tmip)

## 5. Report an accessibility issue

If you encounter an accessibility issue or need content in an alternative format, please contact us and we will respond at earliest.

### Direct Support & Feedback Channels

Our accessibility response team monitors all inbound requests to ensure barrier-free assistance.

Email Feedback [hello@treel.in](mailto:hello@treel.in?subject=Accessibility%20feedback)

Subject line: "Accessibility feedback" helps us route your inquiry quickly to the right engineering team.

Toll-Free Phone Support [1800 833 0233](tel:18008330233)

Toll-free voice support available Monday through Friday, 8:00 AM – 8:00 PM IST.

## 6. Ongoing improvements

Accessibility is not a one-time achievement. We conduct accessibility reviews on every major release and incorporate user feedback into our roadmap.

We continuously evaluate third-party tools, train engineering and design teams on evolving WCAG criteria, and prioritize fixes based on user community input.

LEGAL & COMPLIANCE

# Cookie Policy

Effective October 1, 2026 · Last updated October 1, 2026 · treel.in

### Policy Sections

Quick Action [Button: Manage cookie preferences] 01

## What are cookies

Cookies are small text files placed on your device when you visit a website. They allow the website to remember your actions and preferences (such as language, region, and login state) over time. Similar technologies (local storage, pixels, tags) serve related purposes.

02

## Cookie categories we use

We use four categories of cookies on treel.in:

### 2.1 · Strictly necessary cookies

Always active

Required for the website to function. Cannot be disabled. Include:

- • Session cookies (remember your cart, form inputs)

- • Security cookies (protect against fraud)

- • Cookie consent state (remember your preferences on this page)

### 2.2 · Functional cookies

Opt-in

Enhance your experience but not required. Include:

- • Language and region preferences

- • Recently viewed products

- • Contact form pre-fill (returning visitors)

### 2.3 · Analytics cookies

Opt-in

Help us understand how visitors use the website. Include:

- • Google Analytics (page views, session duration, referrers)

- • Microsoft Clarity (heatmaps, session recordings, anonymised)

We do not use analytics cookies to identify individual visitors. Data is aggregated.

### 2.4 · Marketing cookies

Opt-in

Deliver relevant marketing across our channels. Include:

- • Meta (Facebook, Instagram) advertising pixels

- • Google Ads conversion tracking

- • LinkedIn insight tag

03

## Manage your preferences

You can update your cookie preferences at any time:

- • Through our cookie consent banner — click the "Configure" button when it appears, or use the "Cookie preferences" link in the footer

- • Through your browser settings — most browsers allow you to block or delete cookies; instructions vary by browser

- • Through platform-specific opt-outs — Google Analytics opt-out browser add-on; Meta&#x27;s ad preferences page

[Button: Manage cookie preferences] 04

## Cookies set by third parties

Some cookies are set by third-party services we use (Google Analytics, Meta, Razorpay chat, Magic Flow AI, YouTube video embeds). These third parties have their own privacy and cookie policies which govern their use of cookies.

05

## Changes to this policy

We update this Cookie Policy when we add, remove, or change cookies. The "Last updated" date reflects the current version.

06

<a id="page-8-data-infrastructure"></a>

# [8] Data Infrastructure · Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/data-infrastructure  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/data-infrastructure  
**Meta Description:** *Treel operates India&#x27;s largest connected commercial vehicle dataset. 68,000 vehicles under active management, sub-second telemetry, data from over 2 million tyres. Infrastructure, governance, and privacy.*  

### Page Content

DATA INFRASTRUCTURE

# India&#x27;s largest connected commercial vehicle dataset.

68,412 vehicles under active management. Sub-second telemetry from each. Data from over 2 million tyres. This is the foundation Treel&#x27;s mobility intelligence platform runs on.

## What the numbers mean.

Sixty-eight thousand vehicles report to the Treel platform continuously. Each vehicle generates several million telemetry points per year. Across the fleet, we process billions of measurements. This is not a research dataset. It is an operating dataset that fleets bet their business on every day.

68,412 Vehicles under active management Continuous telemetry reporting from commercial fleets Sub-second Telemetry ingestion latency Near-instantaneous wheel-end sensor ingestion 2M+ Connected tyres monitored Operational telemetry from Indian highways (Founded 2018) Billions Measurements processed annually Operating telemetry fleets bet their business on

## How the infrastructure holds up.

Every Vehicle Digital Twin is persisted independently. State updates are eventually consistent within seconds; history is immutable and append-only. The infrastructure runs on cloud regions with automatic failover. Backup and disaster recovery are exercised quarterly.

The architecture is designed for two properties that matter most to enterprise fleet operators: availability (the platform is up when the fleet needs it, currently 99.7 percent trailing 90-day) and integrity (the data you queried yesterday is the data you queried today; nothing shifts silently).

END-TO-END TELEMETRY PIPELINE 99.7% Trailing Availability 01 · Wheel Sensors Rim and valve transducers measure chamber pressure & heat. 02 · Edge Gateway Store-and-forward buffers maintain continuity in dead zones. 03 · Cloud Stream Sub-second ingestion pipeline with automatic regional failover. 04 · Digital Twin State updates in seconds; immutable, append-only history. 05 · Fleet Action Turnaround dispatch, real-time alerts, and ERP export.

## Data governance.

Every customer&#x27;s data is logically isolated. Access controls are role-based and audited. Retention policies are configured per customer. Right-to-delete requests are processed within the timeframes required by the India DPDP Act 2023 and, for future EU deployments, GDPR. We publish our full [privacy policy](/privacy) and a [security page](/tmip/security) for enterprise buyers who need to complete vendor assessments.

## What we do not do.

### We do not sell customer data.

Not aggregated, not anonymised, not to third parties. Ever.

### We do not train shared competitive models.

We do not use customer data to train models that benefit competing fleets. Model training happens strictly within customer boundaries.

### No mandatory secondary consent.

We do not require customers to consent to secondary use of their data as a condition of using the platform. Consent for research or benchmarking is opt-in, revocable, and separate from the operating contract.

TECHNICAL DOCUMENTATION

## For technical buyers.

For enterprise buyers, engineering teams, or research collaborators who need architecture depth, we publish a technical whitepaper on the data infrastructure.

[ Read the whitepaper ](/research/data-architecture)

## Discuss integration.

Connect directly with our engineering and platform architecture team in Pune to evaluate integration with your enterprise fleet systems.

[ Talk to engineering ](/contact)

<a id="page-10-founder-note"></a>

# [10] What I saw before I started Treel · Manish Ingale, Founder · Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/founder-note  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/founder-note  
**Meta Description:** *Why Manish Ingale founded Treel: from watching a truck driver check tyre pressure with a stick on a Maharashtra highway to operating Vehicle Digital Twins for 68,000 commercial vehicles.*  

### Page Content

I remember the first time I watched a truck driver check his tyre pressure with a stick.

He held the stick in one hand, tapped each tyre with a short, practiced strike, and listened. He was not listening for anything specific. He only wanted the sound to match what he expected. It matched all six times. He was satisfied. He drove off.

That was fleet management in India, one truck at a time, on the side of a highway in Maharashtra. Multiply that moment by nearly 40 lakh heavy trucks and you have the problem I started Treel to solve. Not the stick. The fog.

The fog is what fleet owners knew was there but could not see. They knew which trucks they owned. They knew what they paid for fuel. They knew when a truck broke down. Between those three data points was every operating decision they had to make. They made those decisions with instinct because there was nothing else.

“I had spent enough years in the tyre industry to know that the tyre is the most talkative component in a commercial vehicle. If you can listen to a tyre, you can hear the vehicle. If you can hear the vehicle, you can reason about the fleet.”

We started at the tyre because that is where the conversation began. But we knew from the first day that the tyre was the entry point, not the destination. The destination was always the vehicle. And the vehicle, once you can hear it, becomes something no fleet operator has had before: a queryable object. An intelligence surface. What we now call the Vehicle Digital Twin.

The category had a name for what we were doing back then. They called it tyre monitoring. It was an accurate name for a small idea. What we are actually doing is bigger, and the industry is catching up to the words for it. We call it mobility intelligence, and we call the company that operates it Treel.

We are not the only company in this space. We do not need to be. India&#x27;s commercial vehicle industry is large enough that the category will support several serious players who can move from monitoring to intelligence with discipline and speed. What we are trying to be is the company that defines what mobility intelligence means, ships the platform that operationalises it, and earns the customers who trust it with their fleets.

Two hundred enterprise customers now trust us with sixty-eight thousand vehicles. Our platform is up 99.7 percent of the time. Our median customer sees their investment pay back in nine months. These are not the numbers of an experiment. They are the numbers of a company that has moved from a hypothesis to an operating category.

But this is still Day One. India&#x27;s truck fleet keeps growing every year. Every truck is a signal. Every signal is an insight. Every insight, in the right operator&#x27;s hands, becomes a decision that saves fuel, extends tyre life, prevents a blowout, or gets a shipment to its destination on time.

Treel is an official partner of JK Tyre. That partnership gives us five decades of tyre engineering, national distribution reach, and the operational credibility that only a real industrial partner can lend a young platform company. We operate independently on product, platform, and go-to-market. The relationship works because both sides are clear about what each does best.

If you need clear visibility across a large fleet, and a payback that holds up before you sign, choose [TMIP](/tmip) . We sell only when the numbers work for you. If they do not, we walk away.

If you drive your own truck and need tyre safety in your own language, with no app to download and no monthly fee, choose [Suraksha](/suraksha) . It pays for itself in nine months.

If you need peace of mind on your car&#x27;s tyres, choose [Personal TPMS](/personal) . It runs on the same technology our enterprise customers trust with their fleets.

If you want to build something India has not built before, [join us](/about#team) . We have room for people who want to help build it.

Every vehicle a signal. Every signal an insight. This is what we do at Treel.

Thank you for reading.

Manish Ingale Founder & CEO, Treel Mobility Solutions Pvt. Ltd. · Pune, India

# [11] GDPR · Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/gdpr  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/gdpr  
**Meta Description:** *Treel&#x27;s GDPR readiness for future European Union deployments. Data subject rights, data protection officer contact, and lawful bases for processing.*  

### Page Content

LEGAL

# GDPR

Effective date: 1 October 2026 Jurisdiction: European Union Readiness treel.in/gdpr Current Status & EU Deployment Roadmap

Placeholder page. Full activation on European Union deployment (Month 8+ per current roadmap).

Prior to EU activation, this framework demonstrates architecture and process readiness for enterprise buyers evaluating Treel across European fleets.

Table of Contents DPO Inquiries [ dpo@treel.in](mailto:dpo@treel.in)

## 1. Applicability

The EU General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) governs the processing of personal data of individuals in the European Union. Treel currently operates primarily in India and does not knowingly collect personal data of EU residents through targeted commercial activities.

For future European Union deployments (planned for Month 8+ of our current roadmap), we maintain GDPR-ready architecture, contracts, and processes. This page will be expanded to full detail at that time.

## 2. GDPR Data Subject Rights

If you are an EU resident and believe we may hold your personal data, you have the following rights under GDPR:

1 . Right of access

Request confirmation and a copy of your personal data.

2 . Right to rectification

Request correction of inaccurate data.

3 . Right to erasure

"Right to be forgotten" — request deletion of your data.

4 . Right to restriction of processing

Request temporary halt to processing.

5 . Right to data portability

Request your data in a machine-readable format.

6 . Right to object

Object to processing based on legitimate interests or direct marketing.

7 . Rights related to automated decision-making

Request human review of automated decisions with legal effect.

## 3. Legal Basis for Processing

When Treel processes personal data of EU residents, we rely on one of these lawful bases:

### Consent

User Choice

You have given explicit consent for a specific purpose.

### Contract

Operational

Processing is necessary to perform a contract with you.

### Legal obligation

Mandatory

Processing is required by law.

### Legitimate interests

Balanced

Processing is necessary for our legitimate business interests, balanced against your rights.

## 4. Cross-Border Data Transfer

When we transfer EU personal data outside the European Economic Area, we use appropriate safeguards including Standard Contractual Clauses (SCCs) or reliance on adequacy decisions where available.

Safeguards and Standard Contractual Clauses (SCCs)

Our international infrastructure guarantees that any prospective data transfer outside the EEA is covered by standard contractual protections approved by the European Commission, ensuring persistent data subject rights and comprehensive technical isolation.

## 5. Contact our Data Protection Officer

For GDPR-related requests or concerns:

### Data Protection Officer

Treel Mobility Solutions Private Limited

Electronic Mail [dpo@treel.in](mailto:dpo@treel.in)

Direct mailbox for EU data subject inquiries and rights requests.

Postal Address S.No. 6/1B, 6/4, 7/4, Plot No. 02, Laxmi Vishnupuram Amenities Business, NDA Road, Village Shivane, Tal. Haveli, Pune, Maharashtra 411023

## 6. Right to Lodge a Complaint

If you believe our processing of your personal data violates GDPR, you have the right to lodge a complaint with your local Data Protection Authority.

## Built for Cross-Border Deployment

Commercial transport demands uncompromised reliability across international borders, varied cellular bands, extreme climates, and multi-tier fleet governance. Treel systems are architected from the physical layer up to satisfy global distribution specifications.

### Multi-Band Cellular & RF Telemetry

Support for global 4G LTE-M and 2G fallback frequencies, coupled with industrial 433 MHz RF sensor links, ensuring continuous data transmission across multi-country transit routes.

### Automotive & Data Compliance Standards

Engineered in strict conformity with IATF 16949 (automotive quality standard), GDPR data privacy, ISO 27001 information security, FCC and CE radio/EMC certifications, alongside ECE R141 and IP65 / IP67 environmental sealing specifications.

### Distributed Cloud & Regional Residency

Cloud platform deployment options accommodating regional data sovereignty regulations, low-latency streaming endpoints, and multi-tenant fleet isolation.

### Extreme Climate Envelope

Wheel-end sensor hardware validated for continuous operation from sub-zero refrigerated transit conditions up to high-ambient desert hauling and heavy quarry duty.

Engagement Framework

Treel structures its international market engagement across defined distribution models, OEM factory-fit partnerships, and cross-border commercial fleets.

<a id="page-21-mobility-intelligence"></a>

# [21] What is Mobility Intelligence? · Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/mobility-intelligence  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/mobility-intelligence  
**Meta Description:** *Mobility intelligence is the category that replaces tyre monitoring for fleet operators. It treats every vehicle as a queryable data object. Definition, adoption, and why the category emerged.*  

### Page Content

CATEGORY DEFINITION

# What is mobility intelligence?

Mobility intelligence is the operational category that treats every vehicle as a queryable data object rather than a monitored component.

It replaces the older “tyre monitoring” and “fleet telematics” categories with an architecture built around the Vehicle Digital Twin. Treel is the company that operates this category in India across 68,000+ commercial vehicles.

AT A GLANCE

Mobility intelligence is the category that replaces tyre monitoring and fleet telematics with a Vehicle Digital Twin architecture. Fleet operators reason about vehicles the way software engineers reason about data.

## From tyre monitoring to mobility intelligence.

Ten years ago, the fleet intelligence category was called tyre monitoring. Sensors on tyres reported pressure and temperature. Fleet owners received alerts when a tyre needed attention. It was useful, but small.

Five years ago, the category expanded into fleet telematics. GPS, fuel data, and driver behaviour joined the mix. Fleet owners could see where their trucks were and how they were being driven. Better, but still fragmented across separate tools.

Today, the category has expanded again into mobility intelligence. Every subsystem of every vehicle is represented as a queryable data object. The fleet is no longer a collection of trucks with tyres and fuel sensors; it is a portfolio of Vehicle Digital Twins operating on a shared platform.

The name changed because the architecture changed. And the architecture changed because the operator&#x27;s job changed: from reacting to component alerts, to reasoning about the fleet as a business.

## The mobility intelligence operating model.

How fleet decision-making transforms across three generations of technology:

Stage 1 Legacy

### Reactive

Old-model fleet management was reactive. A tyre blew out; the truck stopped; the fleet operator reacted. The response was correct but expensive: towing fees, delayed shipments, driver downtime.

Response window: Minutes after failure Stage 2 Telematics

### Proactive

Mid-generation fleet telematics moved operators to proactive. Sensors flagged risks before they became failures. Tyre pressure warnings gave a 24-hour window to act. Better, but the operator was still responding to a signal from a single component.

Response window: 24 hours prior Stage 3 Mobility Intelligence

### Predictive

Mobility intelligence enables predictive. Machine learning models forecast component failure days or weeks in advance, based on the full history of the Vehicle Digital Twin. Maintenance happens during planned downtime.

Response window: Days to weeks ahead

## Who operates in this category.

Mobility intelligence adoption in India follows the pattern most enterprise software categories follow: early adopters in logistics and passenger transport lead, followed by construction, mining, and OEM fleets. Treel operates across all five verticals. Two hundred enterprise customers now run on the platform. The category is still in its first decade of scale.

## Treel is the mobility intelligence company.

We are not the only company operating in this category. What we claim, and what our customers validate, is that we operate the platform, own the largest dataset, and are moving fastest to define what mobility intelligence means as an operating discipline. Read our founder note for the fuller story.

[Read the founder note ](/founder-note)

Four-Wheeler & Passenger Car

## Passenger Vehicle Factory-Fit Telematics

Seamless factory integration for passenger sedans, SUVs, and electric cars. Combines high-precision interior rim valve sensors with central gateway ECU protocols, allowing auto manufacturers to provide live tyre pressure and temperature on the instrument cluster without third-party gadgets.

[Request 4W OEM Engineering Whitepaper ](/contact) Bus & Mass Transit

## Public Transit & Intercity Coach Telematics

Passenger transit buses operate under intense duty cycles where a single tyre failure creates extreme schedule disruptions and passenger safety hazards. Treel&#x27;s bus OEM platform monitors dual-wheel rear axles, preventing inner tyre dragging and silent bead blowouts.

- Dual-wheel pressure differential alerts for rear twin axles

- Depot departure gate auto-scan readers verifying tyre compliance

- Direct driver console alert and cloud depot dispatch sync

### Dual-Axle Balance

Detects when an inner tyre loses pressure while the outer tyre carries 100% of the load, preventing sudden highway blowouts.

### Depot Gate Scanner

RFID/BLE roadside portal scans bus tyres at depot exit in 3 seconds, prohibiting non-compliant buses from departing.

### Electric Bus Cooling

Thermal sensors correlate battery pack proximity heating with tyre cavity air temperatures to prevent heat degradation.

### Braking Heat Warning

Flags sticking brake callipers by detecting anomalous wheel hub thermal transfer long before friction causes fire.

Pressure Ceiling

### Up to 200 PSI Pressure Range

High-tensile transducers designed for heavy dumpers, tippers, and open-cast haul trucks operating 24/7.

Mechanical Durability

### 50G Impact & Vibration Rating

Survives aggressive rock-face impacts, gravel blasting, and high-vibration quarry transit corridors.

Thermal Modeling

### TKPH / Ton-Kilometer Per Hour

Calculates carcass internal heat build-up against manufacturer TKPH limits to prevent explosive separations.

Ingress Protection

### IP67 Environmental Enclosure

Complete resistance against high-temperature 100-bar steam cleaning, chemical slurries, and deep quarry mud.

Mining & Heavy OTR

## Extreme Duty Off-Highway & Quarry Equipment

OTR tyres on 100-ton mining dumpers represent significant operating capital expenditures. Treel&#x27;s heavy OTR telemetry prevents thermal blowouts, provides real-time load distribution, and maximizes casing retreadability in harsh mining environments.

Agriculture & Farm Machinery

## Tractor & Harvester Low-Pressure Telemetry

Modern precision agriculture requires precise tyre inflation to prevent soil compaction and maximize field traction. Over-inflation damages crop root systems and reduces yield; under-inflation on tarmac destroys tyre sidewalls. Treel provides real-time pressure guidance tailored for field vs road transitions.

- Ultra-low pressure sensing down to 8 PSI for high-flotation tyres

- Tractor cabin display sync via ISOBUS / CAN protocols

- Fertilizer, liquid manure, and mud chemical resistance

### Soil Compaction Control

Alerts operators when tractor tyres are over-inflated during tillage, protecting agricultural topsoil porosity.

### Field-to-Road Inflation

Dynamic indicators guide farmers on correct tyre pressures when moving from soft muddy fields to high-speed paved roads.

### Tractor Ballast Balance

Detects uneven water-ballasting or implement weight imbalance across left and right rear tractor wheels.

### ISOBUS Certified

Plug-and-play compatibility with OEM tractor virtual terminals (John Deere, Mahindra, New Holland, Tafe).

ASSEMBLY LINE INTEGRATION

## How Treel Integrates on the OEM Production Line

We provide automotive manufacturers with end-to-end tooling, hardware flashing stations, and CAN-bus protocol stacks so sensors are fitted and verified in seconds without slowing down the line.

### 1. Line Pairing Tooling

Handheld optical barcode scanners pair sensor IDs to chassis VIN numbers in under 8 seconds at wheel-mount assembly stations.

### 2. CAN & UDS Diagnostics

End-of-line verification via standard ISO 14229 UDS diagnostic routines. Automatic DTC fault reporting during roll-off testing.

### 3. Secure Cloud OTA

Firmware algorithm updates and calibration curves deployed over-the-air to vehicle telematics units without dealership callbacks.

AUTOMOTIVE ENGINEERING CONSULTING

### Page Content

[Home](/) / [OEM](/oem) / Commercial Vehicle TPMS COMMERCIAL VEHICLE TPMS · FLEET & OEM

# Commercial Vehicle TPMS. Engineered for Heavy Fleets.

Heavy commercial vehicle tyre telematics, multi-axle pressure and temperature monitoring, and digital twin telemetry engineered for medium and heavy truck manufacturers and enterprise fleet operators.

[Inquire for Commercial Fleets ](/contact) [Explore TMIP Enterprise Platform](/tmip) 68,000+ Vehicles Under Management 85%+ Blowout Incident Reduction 5–7% Tyre-Life Extension 9 Months Median Payback Period CAPABILITIES

## Purpose-built for commercial vehicle operations.

From steer tyres to multi-axle trailer tandems, Treel Commercial Vehicle TPMS delivers real-time visibility, thermal warning protection, and actionable maintenance insights.

### Multi-Axle Pressure Monitoring

Continuous high-precision PSI and bar tracking across steer, drive, lift, and multi-axle trailer wheel positions. Identifies under-inflation immediately before it degrades casing integrity or burns excess fuel.

### Tyre Temperature & Blowout Prevention

Direct internal cavity temperature sensing detects early brake drag, wheel hub bearing friction, and thermal buildup well before catastrophic highway blowout or tyre fire occurs.

### Dual-Wheel Differential Alerts

In twin-wheel assemblies, pressure differences cause the larger tyre to carry excess load and scrub prematurely. Real-time differential alerts eliminate uneven tyre wear across dual tyres.

### CAN J1939 Edge Gateway Telemetry

Integrates with the vehicle&#x27;s CAN bus and communicates wirelessly with wheel sensors. Onboard edge storage guarantees zero telemetry loss during cellular dead zones on national highways.

### Predictive Maintenance & Tread Wear

Machine-learning algorithms analyze pressure-temperature curves to project Remaining Useful Life (RUL) and schedule depot rotations before irreversible tread damage sets in.

### True Cost Per Kilometre Tracking

Measures actual tyre performance and wear rates across axle positions, tyre brands, and retread cycles to provide fleet managers with empirical cost-per-kilometre analytics.

ARCHITECTURE

## From wheel sensors to the TMIP mobility platform.

Commercial Vehicle TPMS combines ruggedized vehicle-grade hardware with the cloud intelligence of TMIP to power real-time fleet operations.

01 HARDWARE

### Ruggedized Wheel Sensors

Internal rim-mount and valve-stem sensors engineered for heavy commercial duty cycles. Rated up to 185 PSI / 12.8 bar and 125°C with multi-year battery longevity and severe vibration resistance.

02 TELEMATICS

### Edge Gateway & CAN Bus

In-cabin or chassis-mounted telematics unit connects wirelessly to wheel sensors and interfaces directly with vehicle CAN J1939 networks with offline telemetry caching.

03 PLATFORM

### TMIP Cloud & Digital Twin

Ingests high-frequency telemetry, calculates thermal expansion compensation, and maps every tyre&#x27;s operational state to its live Vehicle Digital Twin.

04 DISPATCH

### Alert Engine & TMS Sync

Dispatches instant alerts for rapid deflation, high temperature spikes, and pressure mismatch to fleet controllers, maintenance teams, and drivers, syncing with TMS and ERP systems.

MULTI-AXLE VERSATILITY

## Supports up to 24 wheel positions per commercial rig.

Whether deploying on a 4x2 distribution truck or a multi-axle bulk cement tanker with articulated trailers, Treel Commercial Vehicle TPMS handles all commercial chassis layouts.

4x2 & 6x2 Haulage 6x4 Heavy Tippers 8x2 & 8x4 Multi-Axle Trucks Tandem & Tri-Axle Trailers Pneumatic Lift Axles Intercity Bus Coaches

### OEM Integration & Fleet Pilots

Treel partners directly with commercial vehicle OEMs for factory-fit pre-delivery integration, and with large enterprise fleets for managed depot retrofits across India.

<a id="page-31-our-story"></a>

# [31] Our Story · Treel Mobility Solutions | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/our-story  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/our-story  
**Meta Description:** *From a JK Tyre R&amp;D idea in 2018 to India&#x27;s largest connected commercial vehicle dataset in 2026. The Treel story, in milestones.*  

### Page Content

OUR STORY

# Built in India. Defining a category.

From our founding in 2018 to India&#x27;s largest connected commercial vehicle dataset. The milestones that got us here.

2018 · Founding & The Core Conviction

### Treel Mobility Solutions Private Limited incorporated on 12 April 2018.

Manish Ingale founds Treel in Pune with the backing of JK Tyre R&D and the conviction that India&#x27;s commercial vehicles are producing more data than anyone is reading. First product: a ruggedized valve-stem tyre sensor. First bet: that the tyre is the entry point to the vehicle, not the destination.

2019 · First Hundred Fleets

### Word-of-mouth adoption across Maharashtra and Gujarat freight routes.

The industry called it &#x27;tyre monitoring&#x27;. Fleet operators called it &#x27;the sensor that told me my truck was going to blow out before it reached the depot&#x27;. Word-of-mouth spread rapidly across logistics hubs.

2020 · ARAI Certification & International Benchmarks

### ARAI automotive certification secured; formal 5-year sensor warranty instituted.

Treel becomes one of the few Indian hardware telematics platforms with official third-party ARAI certification standing behind its sensor claims. Sensor longevity is proven under harsh Indian monsoon and summer road temperatures.

2022 · Enterprise Platform & TMaaS Launch

### Tyre Management as a Service (TMaaS) launches for large fleet operators.

The product moves from isolated hardware sensors to a unified enterprise cloud platform. Fleet owners start managing multi-axle trailers and nationwide depot inventories from a single centralized dashboard.

2024 · Sixty-Eight Thousand Vehicles

### Dataset compounds past 68,000 active commercial vehicles under management.

The dataset becomes Treel&#x27;s deepest moat. Ingesting over 150 million daily telemetry points across 200 enterprise customers, Treel operates India&#x27;s premier commercial vehicle telematics repository.

2025 · Category Definition: Mobility Intelligence

### Evolution from component telematics to the Vehicle Digital Twin framework.

The category outgrows the tyre. Treel introduces the five-layer Vehicle Digital Twin architecture, crystallizing the shift from reactive component alerts to predictive mobility intelligence.

2026 · The Rebrand & Three Product Families

### Launch of Project Suraksha, TMIP Enterprise refresh, and new brand architecture.

One company. Three product families: TMIP for enterprise, Project Suraksha for owner-drivers, and Personal TPMS for consumers. One mission: turn raw physical motion into operating intelligence.

## Day One, still.

India&#x27;s commercial vehicle fleet will grow to three hundred million vehicles in the next decade. Middle Eastern and European deployments follow the Indian launch, without compromising the core reliability engineered for Indian highways. The category is not close to settled. Treel is here for the long build.

## See what we are building today.

Explore how our five-layer Vehicle Digital Twin engine transforms raw wheel dynamics into enterprise operating intelligence.

[ Explore the platform ](/vehicle-digital-twin) [ Meet the team ](/leadership)

### Page Content

FAQS

# Frequently asked questions.

Answers to common questions about Personal TPMS. If you don&#x27;t find what you need, our support team is available to assist you.

[Button: All] [Button: Product] [Button: Installation] [Button: App] [Button: Pricing & purchase] [Button: Warranty] [Button: Support] [Button: What is Personal TPMS? ]

Personal TPMS is a wireless tyre pressure monitoring system for personal vehicles. Four sensors mount on your tyre valve stems and transmit real-time pressure and temperature readings to your phone via Bluetooth.

[Button: How is it different from my car&#x27;s built-in TPMS? ]

Factory TPMS in most cars only alerts you when pressure drops significantly below target. Personal TPMS shows you continuous readings for every tyre on every drive, so you see leading indicators (slow leaks, temperature drift) instead of just outcome alerts.

<a id="page-40-personal-features-alerts"></a>

# [40] Instant Alerts · Personal TPMS | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/personal/features/alerts  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/personal/features/alerts  
**Meta Description:** *Personal TPMS sends instant alerts before a slow leak becomes a puncture. Configurable thresholds. iOS, Android, Apple Watch notifications.*  

### Page Content

FEATURE · ALERTS

# The alert you actually needed.

Personal TPMS alerts arrive before a slow leak becomes a puncture. Before a hot tyre becomes a blowout. Before the OEM TPMS light comes on.

INTELLIGENT THRESHOLDS

## Alert types.

Five distinct telemetry trigger scenarios engineered to prevent roadside breakdowns and tyre structural damage.

### Low Pressure

Slow Leak Warning Trigger: Pressure drops below your configured threshold (default: 10% below target) Channels: Push notification (phone), Apple Watch tap, in-app banner " Front-left tyre at 28 PSI. Target is 32 PSI. Check for slow leak. "

### High Temperature

Thermal Warning Trigger: Tyre temperature exceeds safe operating range (default: 80°C) Channels: Push notification, in-app banner " Rear-right tyre at 82°C. Consider reducing speed and inspecting at next stop. "

### Rapid Pressure Drop

Puncture / Blowout Risk Trigger: Pressure drops rapidly (>2 PSI in 60 seconds) Channels: Immediate push notification with urgency indicator " Rapid pressure drop on front-right tyre. Possible puncture. Pull over safely and inspect. "

### Sensor Battery Low

Maintenance Notice Trigger: Sensor battery estimated to have less than 6 weeks life remaining Channels: In-app banner, one-time notification " Front-left sensor battery low. Replacement kit available on Amazon or via warranty. "

### Sensor Connection Lost

Hardware Offline Trigger: Sensor stops transmitting for more than 5 minutes while driving Channels: In-app banner " Front-right sensor not responding. Check that the sensor is still installed on the valve stem. " USER CUSTOMIZATION

## You configure the thresholds.

Default alert thresholds work for most passenger vehicles. Advanced users can tune thresholds per vehicle if you drive a vehicle with non-standard tyre pressure targets (loaded SUVs, high-performance cars, off-road-modified vehicles).

<a id="page-42-personal-features-battery"></a>

# [42] 2-Year Battery Life · Personal TPMS | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/personal/features/battery  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/personal/features/battery  
**Meta Description:** *Personal TPMS sensors run on replaceable CR1632 batteries with 2 years of continuous operation. Low-battery warning six weeks before replacement is needed.*  

### Page Content

FEATURE · BATTERY

# Two years. No surprises.

Personal TPMS sensors run on replaceable CR1632 batteries. Two years of continuous monitoring per sensor. Six-week advance warning before replacement is needed.

DESIGN PHILOSOPHY

## The number you actually want to know.

A tyre sensor that runs out of battery mid-drive is worse than no tyre sensor at all — because you have stopped monitoring, but you have also stopped paying attention. Personal TPMS is engineered for two-year battery life so you can pay attention every day for two years, with a clear warning window before replacement.

2 Yrs

### Continuous Monitoring

Standard CR1632 user-replaceable coin cells

Ultra-efficient custom Bluetooth 5.0 Low Energy micro-controller Available on Amazon, Flipkart, or any local electronics store DIY 5-minute swap with zero specialized tools

### How we get two years out of a CR1632.

The sensors go into low-power mode when the car is stationary. They wake automatically when they detect motion. This "monitor while driving, sleep while parked" pattern is what makes two-year battery life possible on a standard CR1632.

### The six-week warning window.

When a sensor battery reaches an estimated six weeks of remaining life, the app raises a one-time low-battery warning. You have six weeks to replace the battery (or replace the sensor kit if the battery seal has aged out). No mid-drive surprises.

MAINTENANCE

## How to replace a battery.

CR1632 batteries are available at any electronics retailer or on Amazon and Flipkart. Estimated time: 5 minutes per sensor.

01

### Unscrew from valve

Remove the sensor and anti-theft hex nut from the tyre valve stem using the provided spanner.

02

### Open housing

Open the lightweight aluminum sensor housing with a small Phillips screw on the base.

03

### Swap coin cell

Remove the depleted CR1632 cell and slide in the new standard battery with the (+) positive side facing upward.

04

### Reseal & mount

Ensure the silicone O-ring is seated snugly. Close the shell and screw the sensor firmly back onto the valve.

05

### Automatic re-pairing

The sensor wakes and reconnects automatically with the Treel mobile app within 30 seconds.

LONG-TERM DURABILITY

## When to replace the whole sensor.

After 3–4 battery cycles (6–8 years), the sensor&#x27;s internal seals age out. At that point, replace the whole sensor. Replacement sensor sets are available through Treel warranty or direct purchase.

[Warranty details →](/personal/support/warranty)

<a id="page-43-personal-features-monitoring"></a>

# [43] Real-Time Monitoring · Personal TPMS | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/personal/features/monitoring  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/personal/features/monitoring  
**Meta Description:** *Personal TPMS updates tyre pressure and temperature every three seconds when driving. Continuous visibility, not just alerts.*  

### Page Content

FEATURE · MONITORING

# Continuous visibility. Not just alerts.

Personal TPMS updates every tyre reading every three seconds when you&#x27;re driving. You see what your tyres are doing in real time — not just when something is wrong.

BLUETOOTH LOW ENERGY

## How the monitoring works.

Every sensor takes a pressure and temperature reading every three seconds while the car is moving. The reading is transmitted to your phone over Bluetooth Low Energy. The app updates the display continuously. No refresh, no polling, no delay.

When the car is parked, the sensors go into low-power mode to preserve battery life. They wake automatically the moment you start driving.

### 3-Second Refresh

Continuous Bluetooth Low Energy telemetry broadcast while in motion.

### Smart Sleep Mode

Sensors enter deep hibernation when stationary to maximize 2-year cell life.

### Instant Motion Wake

Internal centrifugal accelerometers detect wheel rotation and wake in milliseconds.

### 30-Day Trip Logs

Historical pressure and thermal behavior saved locally for retrospective diagnostics.

UNIFIED DASHBOARD

## The app view.

The Personal TPMS app shows all four tyres on a single screen. Each tyre displays exact pressure, temperature, trend direction, and battery health. Trip history is retained for the last 30 days. You can see how tyre pressures behaved on any drive in the last month.

### Current Pressure

Instant live pressure in PSI or kPa (user selectable) with ±0.5 PSI precision.

### Current Temperature

Real-time internal tyre cavity temperature in °C across all four wheels.

### Trend Indicator

Visual direction indicator showing whether pressure is rising, stable, or dropping.

### Sensor Battery Status

Exact battery percentage for each wheel&#x27;s CR1632 coin cell.

REAL-WORLD UTILITY

## What continuous monitoring lets you see.

High Speed Transit

### On a highway drive

Watch tyre temperatures climb as ambient temperature rises. Notice one tyre running 3–4°C hotter than the others. Investigate at the next stop.

Maintenance Verification

### After a service visit

Verify all four tyres were inflated to the same target pressure. Not the pressure written on the invoice; the actual pressure in the tyres.

Pre-Drive Check

### Before a long trip

Confirm all tyres are at the recommended cold pressure before you leave. See the reading before you drive, not the alert after.

Post-Repair Assurance

### After a puncture repair

Monitor the repaired tyre continuously for the next few drives to confirm the seal is holding.

[Contact support ](/personal/support/contact) FREQUENT ANSWERS

## Common questions

Quick answers to the most common queries from personal car drivers.

### How long does installation take?

10 minutes for a typical car. The sensors screw onto valve stems finger-tight; no tools required. Follow the setup guide or watch the video.

### Will it damage my tyres or valve stems?

No. Personal TPMS sensors are lightweight (8 grams each) and mount using the same threading as a standard valve cap. They do not damage tyres or valve stems.

### How is it different from my car&#x27;s built-in TPMS?

Factory TPMS in most cars only alerts you when pressure drops significantly below target. Personal TPMS shows you continuous readings for every tyre on every drive, so you see leading indicators (slow leaks, temperature drift) instead of just outcome alerts.

### Are the sensors safe at highway speeds?

Yes. Personal TPMS sensors are tested at speeds up to 200 km/h. The lightweight design and locking rings ensure they stay in place at any legal highway speed.

### What is covered under the 1-year warranty?

1-year full replacement warranty on all four sensors and the app. Covers manufacturing defects and Bluetooth hardware failure. Replacement dispatched within 48 hours.

# [53] Setup Guide · Personal TPMS | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/personal/support/setup

**Meta Description:** *Install Personal TPMS in 10 minutes. Six-step guide with photos and video. No tools required beyond what&#x27;s in the box.*  

### Page Content

SETUP GUIDE

# Installed in 10 minutes.

Personal TPMS is designed for DIY installation. Six steps. Video below if you prefer to watch.

[

### 3-Minute Installation Walkthrough

Watch our quick visual guide demonstrating sensor mounting, anti-theft locking, and mobile pairing.

](https://youtu.be/tYUgbtc8Aik) STEP-BY-STEP

## The six steps

01

### Download the app

Install the Personal TPMS app from the App Store or Google Play. Open the app and create your profile (name and email — used only for warranty).

iOS 15+ / Android 10+ 02

### Add your vehicle

In the app, tap "Add vehicle." Enter your vehicle make, model, and target tyre pressure (find this on the driver-side door jamb sticker or your owner&#x27;s manual).

OEM Door Placard Pressure 03

### Unpack the sensors

Your kit includes four sensors, labeled FL (front-left), FR (front-right), RL (rear-left), RR (rear-right). Keep them in position order for the next step.

FL, FR, RL, RR Position Marked 04

### Install the sensors

For each tyre: unscrew the valve cap, screw the corresponding sensor onto the valve stem (finger-tight, no tools needed), and lock it with the included anti-theft ring.

Anti-Theft Brass Lock Nut 05

### Pair with the app

Return to the app. Tap "Pair sensors." Drive your car forward and backward for 30 seconds (this wakes the sensors). Sensors auto-pair with the app in position order.

Automatic Bluetooth 5.0 Handshake 06

### You&#x27;re done

All four tyres now show live pressure and temperature on your app home screen. Drive normally. The app will alert you if anything changes.

Continuous Live Dashboard Active

### Something not working?

If a sensor doesn&#x27;t pair, if the app doesn&#x27;t recognise a sensor, or if you see other issues during setup, see the troubleshooting page.

### Page Content

TROUBLESHOOTING

# Something not working? Let&#x27;s fix it.

Common Personal TPMS issues and their solutions. If your issue isn&#x27;t listed here or the solution doesn&#x27;t work, contact support.

## Common Problems & Fixes

Click on any issue below to see diagnostic steps.

[Button: A sensor won&#x27;t pair with the app ] Probable Cause: The sensor may not be waking from low-power mode, or Bluetooth connection is intermittent. Step-by-step Solution:

- Confirm the sensor is properly installed on the valve stem (finger-tight).

- Drive the car forward and backward for 30 seconds — motion wakes the sensor.

- In the app, tap "Retry pairing" for the specific position.

- Ensure your phone&#x27;s Bluetooth is on and Bluetooth permission is granted to the app.

- If still failing, remove the sensor, inspect for damage, and reinstall.

[Button: The app shows one tyre as "disconnected" ] [Button: The app shows pressure readings that seem incorrect ] [Button: I&#x27;m not receiving alerts ] [Button: A sensor came loose while driving ] [Button: The app crashes or won&#x27;t open ]

## Still stuck?

If your issue isn&#x27;t listed above or the solutions don&#x27;t work, our support team is ready to help.

# 1 year. Full replacement. No questions.

Every Personal TPMS kit ships with a 1-year full replacement warranty on all four sensors and the app. Register below, or submit a claim if something has gone wrong.

## Coverage Summary

- Sensor manufacturing defects — Full replacement of affected sensor(s).

- App connectivity issues — Free updates and priority support to resolve.

- Sensor housing integrity — Full replacement if IP67 seal fails under normal driving conditions.

- Coverage duration — 12 months from the date of purchase.

## Not Covered

- Physical damage from severe impact, crushing, kerbing, or misuse.

- Water damage below or beyond the specified IP67 rating.

- Battery replacement (CR1632 is a standard consumable, easily replaced).

- Damage resulting from unauthorized tampering, disassembly, or third-party repair.

- Loss or theft of individual sensors.

# [58] Privacy Policy  ·  Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/privacy  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/privacy  
**Meta Description:** *Treel&#x27;s privacy policy. How we collect, use, share, and protect your personal data. Compliant with India DPDP Act 2023.*  

### Page Content

DATA PROTECTION & PRIVACY

# Privacy Policy

Effective October 1, 2026 · Last updated October 1, 2026 · treel.in Table of Contents

## 1. Introduction

Treel Mobility Solutions Private Limited (referred to as "Treel", "we", "us", "our") is committed to protecting the privacy of individuals who interact with our website (treel.in), our products (TMIP, Project Suraksha, Personal TPMS), and our services.

This Privacy Policy explains how we collect, use, share, retain, and protect personal data in accordance with the India Digital Personal Data Protection Act 2023 (the "DPDP Act") and other applicable Indian laws.

By using treel.in or our products and services, you consent to the collection and use of your information in accordance with this policy.

## 2. Information we collect

### 2.1 Information you provide directly

We collect personal data you provide when you:

- Request a product demo or trial

- Purchase a Treel product (through treel.in or through a Truck Wheels centre)

- Submit an enquiry through a contact form

- Apply for financing through our Bajaj Finance partnership

- Subscribe to our newsletter or blog updates

- Register for warranty or claim a warranty

- Interact with our customer support team

- Apply for employment

The categories of personal data collected typically include:

- Name, email address, phone number, mobile number

- Company name, role, industry (for enterprise contacts)

- Delivery address, billing address (for e-commerce transactions)

- Vehicle registration number and truck details (for Suraksha and TMIP)

- Aadhar, PAN, and financial documents (for EMI applications, per Bajaj Finance requirements)

- Payment details (processed by Razorpay; we do not store payment card data)

- Any other information you voluntarily submit

### 2.2 Information collected automatically

When you use treel.in, we collect certain information automatically through cookies, log files, and analytics tools:

- IP address, device type, browser type, operating system

- Pages visited, time spent, navigation patterns

- Referring URLs and outbound clicks

- Approximate geographic location (city/state level)

### 2.3 Information from Treel product usage

When you use a Treel product (TMIP, Suraksha, or Personal TPMS), the product generates operational data about the connected vehicle:

- Tyre pressure, temperature, and related sensor readings

- Vehicle location (only for products with GPS-enabled telematics)

- Driver behaviour indicators (for TMIP enterprise deployments)

- Usage patterns and system health metrics

This operational data is stored in your Vehicle Digital Twin and used to deliver the product&#x27;s core functionality.

## 3. How we use your information

We use personal data for the following purposes:

- • To provide our products and services — fulfilling orders, delivering Vehicle Digital Twin functionality, generating alerts, running the ROI calculator, providing customer support.

- • To process transactions — payment processing, EMI applications, shipment coordination, warranty claims, refunds.

- • To communicate with you — order confirmations, tracking updates, warranty notifications, support responses, service updates.

- • To improve our products and services — analysing aggregated usage patterns, identifying product issues, training predictive models on operational data (within customer boundaries).

- • To market our products — with your consent, sending you information about new features, whitepapers, events, and product updates.

- • To comply with legal obligations — statutory reporting, regulatory compliance, responding to lawful requests from government authorities.

- • To protect our rights and safety — preventing fraud, enforcing our terms of service, protecting our intellectual property.

## 4. How we share your information

We share personal data only in the following circumstances:

### 4.1 With service providers

We share data with third-party service providers who help us operate our business:

- Razorpay for payment processing

- Shiprocket for shipping and logistics

- Bajaj Finance for EMI applications and financing

- Magic Flow AI for our website chatbot

- Cloud infrastructure providers for hosting Treel platform infrastructure

- Analytics providers (Google Analytics, and privacy-first alternatives)

All service providers are bound by contract to protect your data and use it only for the purposes we specify.

### 4.2 Strategic & Corporate Partners

Treel operates independently but may share aggregated, non-personal insights with corporate partners for governance and reporting purposes. We do not share individual customer personal data with external entities for marketing, sales, or any other secondary purpose.

### 4.3 With our authorized dealer and installation network

For Suraksha customers, we share the minimum necessary contact information with authorized fitment centres or certified puncture shops in connection with installation, warranty service, or support requests you initiate.

### 4.4 With legal or regulatory authorities

We share personal data when required by law, court order, or lawful government request. We evaluate every request for legality and scope before disclosure.

### 4.5 In business transfers

If Treel is involved in a merger, acquisition, or asset sale, personal data may be transferred as part of that transaction. We will notify affected users through our website and by email where reasonably possible.

We do not sell your personal data. We have never sold personal data, and we do not plan to.

## 5. Data retention

We retain personal data only for as long as necessary to fulfil the purposes described in this policy, comply with legal obligations, resolve disputes, and enforce our agreements.

Typical retention periods:

- • Transactional records (orders, invoices, warranty registrations) — 8 years, per statutory requirement

- • Customer support records — 3 years from case closure

- • Marketing preferences — until you unsubscribe or object

- • Website analytics — 26 months (Google Analytics default; longer if required for legitimate business analysis)

- • Vehicle Digital Twin operational data — as long as the vehicle is under active Treel platform management, plus 12 months post-termination for statutory or dispute purposes

You can request earlier deletion where permitted by law (see Section 6).

## 6. Your rights under DPDP Act 2023

As a data principal under the India Digital Personal Data Protection Act 2023, you have the following rights:

Right to access

Request confirmation of whether we process your personal data, and a copy of the data we hold.

Right to correction

Request correction of inaccurate or incomplete data.

Right to erasure

Request deletion of your personal data, subject to statutory retention requirements.

Right to grievance redressal

Escalate unresolved concerns directly to our Data Protection Officer.

Right to nominate

Nominate another individual to exercise your rights in the event of death or incapacity.

Right to withdraw consent

Withdraw consent for processing based on consent at any time.

To exercise any of these rights, contact our Data Protection Officer at [dpo@treel.in](mailto:dpo@treel.in) . We will respond within the timeframes required by DPDP Act 2023 (typically within 30 days of a validated request).

## 7. Data security

We implement appropriate technical and organizational measures to protect personal data:

- Encryption in transit (TLS 1.3) and at rest (AES-256)

- Role-based access controls with multi-factor authentication for privileged access

- Logical isolation of every customer&#x27;s data

- Regular penetration testing by third-party security firms

- 99.7% platform uptime (trailing 90-day rolling average)

- Incident response processes with customer notification within contractual SLA

Despite our efforts, no method of transmission or storage is 100% secure. We commit to acting in good faith and with due diligence to protect your data.

## 8. Cookies and tracking technologies

We use cookies and similar technologies to operate our website, remember your preferences, analyse usage, and (with your consent) deliver relevant marketing. See our [Cookie Policy](/cookies) for full details, cookie categories, and how to manage your preferences.

## 9. International data transfers

Treel is headquartered in Pune, India, and processes personal data primarily within India. For future international expansion (Middle East and Europe), we will maintain regional data residency where required by local law. Any international transfer will follow the safeguards mandated by DPDP Act 2023 and relevant destination jurisdictions.

## 10. Children&#x27;s privacy

Our website and products are not directed to children under 18. We do not knowingly collect personal data from children. If you believe we have inadvertently collected data from a child, please contact us and we will delete it.

## 11. Changes to this policy

We may update this Privacy Policy from time to time. Material changes will be notified through the website and, where reasonably possible, by email to affected users. The "Effective date" and "Last updated" fields at the top of this policy indicate the current version. Prior versions are available on request.

## 12. Contact our Data Protection Officer

For questions, concerns, or requests related to your personal data:

### Data Protection Officer

Treel Mobility Solutions Private Limited

Email: [dpo@treel.in](mailto:dpo@treel.in)

Postal: S.No. 6/1B, 6/4, 7/4, Plot No. 02, Laxmi Vishnupuram Amenities Business, NDA Road, Village Shivane, Tal. Haveli, Pune, Maharashtra 411023

# [60] OTR TPMS for Mining, Construction and Ports | Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/products/otr-tpms  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/products/otr-tpms  
**Meta Description:** *Tyre pressure monitoring built for mining, quarry and port fleets. OTR TPMS sensors feed TMIP, which predicts tyre failures before they stop production.*  

### Page Content

OTR TPMS | Powered by TMIP

# Where the road ends, the intelligence doesn&#x27;t.

Mining haulers, earthmovers and port equipment work under loads and heat that road fleets never see. OTR TPMS puts a sensor on every tyre, and TMIP turns those readings into predictions, so failures don&#x27;t stop the shift.

[Book a site assessment](/tmip/demo) [See how it works](#how) Mining Haul fleets Construction Earthmoving Quarries Loaders, dumpers Ports Terminal equipment

## Off-road, a single tyre can halt a production line.

HEAT + LOAD

### Heat and load

Heavy payloads, long haul cycles and steep ramps build heat inside the tyre faster than any manual check can catch.

IMPACT

### Harsh ground

Rock cuts, impacts and underinflation cause sudden sidewall and casing failures that end a tyre&#x27;s life early.

UPTIME

### Downtime costs production

When a hauler stops, the loader, the crusher and the schedule stop with it.

SAFETY

### Safety at the machine

Failures on large OTR tyres put operators and ground crew at serious risk. Catching them early is a safety outcome, not only a cost outcome.

## From tyre checks at the workshop to predictive intelligence at the pit.

Most sites still rely on scheduled inspections and operator reports. OTR TPMS replaces periodic checks with continuous monitoring, and TMIP learns each machine&#x27;s normal behaviour and flags drift before it becomes a failure.

### How most sites run today

- Scheduled inspections, days or weeks apart

- Issues reported by the operator, if noticed

- Failure found after the machine stops

### With OTR TPMS and TMIP

- Continuous pressure and temperature on every tyre

- A learned baseline for every machine

- Failure predicted before the shift is lost

OTR TPMS | The hardware

## A sensor on every tyre. A reading every few seconds.

OTR TPMS is the physical system on the machine. It measures every tyre continuously and hands the data to TMIP.

Tyre sensor → On-machine gateway → TMIP

- 01

### Pressure and temperature

Continuous pressure and temperature at every tyre position, including inner and outer rear duals.

- 02

### Built for OTR tyres

Sensors built for OTR tyre sizes and mounted to stay in place through impacts and heat.

- 03

### On-machine gateway

A gateway on each machine collects every sensor reading and sends it to TMIP.

- 04

### Alerts in the cab

Instant alerts for the operator when a tyre moves outside its safe range.

- 05

### Digital Tire Management Inspection Tools

Bluetooth-enabled tread depth and pressure digital inspection tools. Workshop and yard technicians record millimeter-precise groove measurements and calibrated cold pressures, syncing live to TMIP to automate retread scheduling and casing scrap analysis.

TMIP | The platform

## Readings become predictions.

### Live site view

Every sensor reading on one live view, with alerts to the supervisor and the operator cab.

### Predictive failure alerts

Remaining useful life and breakdown probability for every tyre and component.

### Fleet health score

One view of which machines are fit for the next shift.

### Operator behaviour

Driver score covering overloading, overspeed on ramps and harsh operation.

### Maintenance planning

A component-level view for the workshop, so tyre rotation and replacement are planned, not reactive.

[Explore maintenance planning →](/tmip/features/maintenance)

### Machine baselines

TMIP learns each vehicle&#x27;s normal pattern and flags anomalies early.

## Engineered for dust, heat, vibration and patchy networks.

### Sensor durability

Sensors rated for OTR tyre sizes and the dust, heat and vibration of active sites.

### Works offline

Data is stored on the machine when the network drops and syncs when it returns.

### Fits your site systems

Works alongside the dispatch and ERP systems already running your site.

## Built for the fleets that work off the highway.

### Mining

Coal, iron ore, and heavy mineral extraction haul fleets.

What matters most trips per shift

### Construction & Earthmovers

Excavators, wheeled loaders, graders, heavy dumpers, and tipper fleets.

What matters most project cycle uptime

### Quarries

Aggregate haulers, stone quarries, front-end loaders, and articulated dumpers.

What matters most operating cost per hour

### Ports & Terminals

Terminal tractors, reach stackers, RTG cranes, and container-handling equipment.

### Cement and steel plants

In-plant logistics vehicles.

## Questions site teams ask.

Which OTR tyre sizes does OTR TPMS fit? Supported tyre sizes and wheel positions are evaluated and confirmed during the initial site walkthrough and fleet audit. Does OTR TPMS work at remote sites with limited network coverage? Data is stored on the machine when the network drops and syncs when it returns. How is OTR TPMS different from a standard TPMS? A standard TPMS shows pressure readings. OTR TPMS sends every reading to TMIP, which combines tyre, engine and operator data, learns each machine&#x27;s baseline and predicts failures before they happen. Do I need TMIP to use OTR TPMS? OTR TPMS is the physical sensor system on the machine that measures every tyre continuously and hands the data to TMIP. Which OTR machines can OTR TPMS be fitted to? OTR TPMS is built for off-highway fleets including mining haulers, earthmovers, loaders, dumpers, and port terminal equipment. How long does deployment take for a site fleet? Sensors and gateways are fitted with minimal disruption to shifts, scheduled around your active site operations. Can alerts reach both supervisors and operators? Yes. Alerts go to the site dashboard and can be routed to supervisors and the operator cab.

### Page Content

DATA INFRASTRUCTURE

# Data Architecture & Telemetry Ingestion at Enterprise Scale

Published Q3 2026 28 pages By Treel Research

## What this paper covers

An architectural deep dive into the engineering foundations powering India&#x27;s largest connected commercial vehicle dataset. Based on longitudinal empirical telemetry across 68,000+ active commercial vehicles, this paper details the distributed pipeline required to ingest, validate, and reason over sub-second sensor signals in real time.

We explore edge store-and-forward resilience across cellular dead zones on Indian freight highways, dual-state database persistence separating queryable hot digital twins from immutable append-only telemetry history, tenant cryptographic isolation, and regulatory compliance under the India DPDP Act 2023.

ARCHITECTURE STACK OVERVIEW TIER 1 Edge Buffer Offline telemetry cache & flash de-duplication TIER 2 Stream Engine Sub-second message broker with clock correction TIER 3 Digital Twin Dual-state persistence & append-only history

### In this paper

- 01 High-Throughput Edge Ingestion: Cellular Disruption & Store-and-Forward Buffering

- 02 Event Stream Architecture: De-duplication, Clock Drift Correction & Serialization

- 03 Dual-State Persistence: Live Digital Twin Models vs. Immutable Historical Logs

- 04 Multi-Tenant Data Isolation & Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)

- 05 Regulatory Compliance: India DPDP Act 2023, Automated Redaction & Audit Trails

- 06 Disaster Recovery & Regional Failover: Achieving 99.7% Availability

# The Fleet ROI and Fuel Savings Paper

Published October 2026 24 pages By Treel Research

## What this paper covers

A comprehensive financial and operational analysis of mobility intelligence and real-time tyre telemetry across commercial vehicle fleets in India. Based on longitudinal empirical data from 200+ enterprise deployments and 68,000 connected vehicles under active management.

This paper breaks down the exact mechanics of 0.5–0.6% fuel savings, 5–7% tyre life extension, and 30–40% reduction in unscheduled downtime, validating the 9-month median capital payback for enterprise fleet operators.

- 06 Enterprise TMS Integration & Implementation Architecture

# EMI STARTS AT ₹1,500 A MONTH.

आसान किस्तों में आज ही सुरक्षा लगवाइए। എളുപ്പമുള്ള തവണകളിൽ ഇന്നുതന്നെ സുരക്ഷ ഘടിപ്പിക്കൂ. आसान किस्तों में आज ही सुरक्षा लगवाइए। સરળ હપ્તામાં આજે જ સુરક્ષા લગાવો. எளிய தவணைகளில் இன்றே சுரக்ஷா பொருத்துங்கள். సులభ వాయిదాలలో ఈరోజే సురక్ష అమర్చుకోండి. ಸುಲಭ ಕಂತುಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಇಂದೇ ಸುರಕ್ಷಾ ಅಳವಡಿಸಿ. ਆਸਾਨ ਕਿਸ਼ਤਾਂ ਵਿੱਚ ਅੱਜ ਹੀ ਸੁਰਕਸ਼ਾ ਲਗਵਾਓ। সহজ কিস্তিতে আজই সুরক্ষা লাগান।

## WHEN IS ZERO-COST EMI AVAILABLE?

Zero-cost EMI is available on selected 9-month plans, meaning the total amount payable equals the kit price with zero interest charges. Limited-time offer, terms apply.

## ELIGIBILITY CRITERIA (Bajaj Finance)

Age 21–65 years Employment Self-employed (truck owner) or salaried Truck ownership Registered owner with valid RC Income proof Bank statement (last 6 months) or ITR CIBIL score 700+ preferred, lower scores considered case-by-case KYC Aadhaar + PAN mandatory

Note: Final eligibility is subject to partner finance approval. Verifications are typically completed within 24–48 hours.

## DOCUMENTS REQUIRED

- Aadhaar card (front and back)

- PAN card

- Truck Registration Certificate (RC)

- Bank statement (last 6 months) or ITR (last 2 years)

- Recent passport-size photo

- Address proof (if different from Aadhaar)

## HOW TO APPLY

1

### Keep Documents Ready

Aadhaar, PAN, RC, and 6-month bank statement.

2

### Complete Application

Fill our quick online form or get assisted at any Truck Wheels centre.

3

### Submit Documents

Upload photos online or share directly via WhatsApp.

4

### Finance Verification

Approval decision in 24–48 hours.

5

### Collect and Fit Kit

Pickup and 15-minute fitment at your nearest centre.

[Apply Now ](/suraksha/emi/apply)

## EMI questions

### Can I get EMI approval with a lower CIBIL score?

Yes. Financing applications are reviewed on a holistic case-by-case basis including vehicle profile and bank statements. Submit your application to check eligibility.

### How much down payment is required?

Zero down payment options are available for eligible applicants. Standard plans typically require 10–20% down payment.

### How long does approval take?

Usually within 24–48 hours. When all clear documents are submitted upfront, same-day approval is often possible.

### What happens if an EMI payment is missed?

Standard finance partner late charges apply. If you foresee any payment delays, contact our helpline on WhatsApp so our team can assist you.

### Can I foreclose or prepay the EMI loan early?

Yes. Early foreclosure is available under standard finance terms with minimal processing charges.

HOW IT WORKS

# FIFTEEN MINUTES. THAT&#x27;S ALL IT TAKES.

फिट करवाइए, चालू कीजिए और चलाइए। ഫിറ്റ് ചെയ്യൂ, ഓൺ ചെയ്യൂ, ഓടിക്കൂ. फिट करवाइए, चालू कीजिए और चलाइए। ફિટ કરાવો, ચાલુ કરો અને ચલાવો. பொருத்துங்கள், ஆன் செய்யுங்கள், ஓட்டுங்கள். ఫిట్ చేయించండి, ఆన్ చేయండి, నడపండి. ಫಿಟ್ ಮಾಡಿಸಿ, ಆನ್ ಮಾಡಿ, ಓಡಿಸಿ. ਫਿੱਟ ਕਰਵਾਓ, ਚਾਲੂ ਕਰੋ ਅਤੇ ਚਲਾਓ। ফিট করান, চালু করুন আর চালান।

Installing Suraksha is simple. No mechanic is required. Visit a puncture shop or Truck Wheels Centre and the installation can be completed in fifteen minutes.

### MONITOR IN REAL TIME

The in-cabin display continuously shows tyre pressure and temperature. If an issue develops, the display beeps and alerts you. No app or subscription required.

Time: Every drive IN-CAB EXPERIENCE

## WHAT THE DISPLAY SHOWS

Continuous tyre telemetry right in front of the driver.

ALL OK · TYRE TELEMETRY ACTIVE SURAKSHA DISPLAY v1 Front Left (FL) 120 PSI 42°C · Normal Front Right (FR) 120 PSI 41°C · Normal Rear Left (RL) 125 PSI 44°C · Normal Rear Right (RR) 125 PSI 45°C · Normal Alert History: No active faults Safe Operation Mode Top Row (System Status)

ALL OK (green) or alert messages (red) when pressure or temperature needs attention.

Center (Vehicle Layout)

Truck outline with 4 tyre positions mapping exact physical wheel placement on the road.

Each Tyre Telemetry

Real-time pressure (PSI), temperature (°C), and live status colour (green / yellow / red).

Bottom Row (Alert History)

Event memory tracking the last 3 critical alert events for easy post-trip review.

ALERT SYSTEM

## CRITICAL ALERT TYPES

PRESSURE LOW Trigger: Tyre pressure drops below safe threshold Display shows: Red flashing on affected tyre + audio alert Action: Pull over safely and inspect the tyre TEMPERATURE HIGH Trigger: Tyre temperature exceeds safe range (85°C+) Display shows: Orange flashing + warning tone Action: Reduce speed. Allow tyre to cool at next stop RAPID DEFLATION Trigger: Rapid pressure drop (puncture in progress) Display shows: Red flashing + continuous urgent alarm Action: Stop immediately and pull off the roadway CARE & MAINTENANCE

## MAINTENANCE BEST PRACTICES

Simple care guidelines for peak reliability.

- • Reassign sensors to their correct wheel positions whenever tyres are rotated

- • If road mud accumulates on sensors, gently wipe clean with a damp cloth

- • Protect the in-cabin display from continuous harsh direct sunlight when parked

- • Replace sensors within the 3-year replacement window before batteries deplete

- • If you ever need assistance, send a photo via WhatsApp and our support team will guide you

QUESTIONS

## Common questions

Does Suraksha work with any tyre brand?

Yes. Suraksha works with any commercial tyre brand, including MRF, Apollo, CEAT, Bridgestone, Michelin, and all standard truck tyres.

Do sensors need to be reinstalled after tyre rotation?

No. The sensors remain on the wheels. Simply update the wheel positions on the display or swap the sensor caps to match.

Is support available on WhatsApp?

Yes. WhatsApp support provides 24/7 automated assistance, with human specialists available during business hours. Toll-free phone support is also available.

How many trucks can use a single kit?

Each kit is engineered for one truck. For multiple trucks, separate kits are required. Bulk fleet pricing is available via WhatsApp or our sales desk.

Are replacement sensors available if lost or stolen?

Yes. Manufacturing defects are covered under the 3-year warranty with free replacement. For accidental physical loss or theft, individual replacement sensors can be purchased separately.
