SAFETY

# THE WARNING BEFORE THE BLOWOUT.

हर टायर पर नज़र, हर सफ़र सुरक्षित। ഓരോ ടയറിലും ശ്രദ്ധ, ഓരോ യാത്രയും സുരക്ഷിതം. हर टायर पर नज़र, हर सफ़र सुरक्षित। દરેક ટાયર પર નજર, દરેક સફર સુરક્ષિત. ஒவ்வொரு டயர் மீதும் கவனம், ஒவ்வொரு பயணமும் பாதுகாப்பு. ప్రతి టైర్‌పై నిఘా, ప్రతి ప్రయాణం సురక్షితం. ಪ್ರತಿ ಟೈರ್ ಮೇಲೆ ನಿಗಾ, ಪ್ರತಿ ಪ್ರಯಾಣ ಸುರಕ್ಷಿತ. ਹਰ ਟਾਇਰ &#x27;ਤੇ ਨਜ਼ਰ, ਹਰ ਸਫ਼ਰ ਸੁਰੱਖਿਅਤ। প্রতিটি টায়ারে নজর, প্রতিটি যাত্রা নিরাপদ।

A front-tyre blowout on an Indian highway can cost a human life and shatter a family&#x27;s sole livelihood. Suraksha continuously tracks the pressure and temperature of every tyre, alerting drivers 24–48 hours before failure so they can stop safely, save crucial transit time, and protect their vehicle.

DETECTION SYSTEM

## WHAT SURAKSHA DETECTS

Three categories with examples:

Category 1 · Slow leaks: What: Pressure gradually drops over hours (nail puncture, valve leak, sidewall crack) Warning window: Usually 12-48 hours before critical failure Display shows: Yellow pressure indicator, gradual decrease alert Category 2 · Temperature spikes: What: Tyre temperature rises abnormally (overloading, prolonged high-speed driving, internal damage, brake dragging) Warning window: Immediate — as temperature rises, display shows warning Display shows: Orange or red temperature indicator, high-temp beep Category 3 · Rapid deflation: What: Sudden pressure drop (large puncture, valve failure, sidewall cut) Warning window: Seconds to minutes before complete tyre failure Display shows: Red flashing alert, continuous alarm — pull over immediately STATISTICS

## KEY SAFETY METRICS

Critical highway data that every commercial vehicle operator should know:

40%

of truck accidents in India involve tyre failures (Source: Ministry of Road Transport & Highways)

80%

of tyre failures show warning signs 24-48 hours in advance

60 km/h

is the typical loaded truck highway speed where a blowout becomes life-threatening

60%

of tyre-related fatalities are preventable with monitoring (industry estimates)

Suraksha pays for itself in 9–12 months through fuel savings, longer tyre life and fewer roadside breakdowns. Payback varies by application and fleet operation. Use the calculator below to see the savings for your truck.

SAVINGS BREAKDOWN

## WHERE THE SAVINGS COME FROM

Three savings buckets with detailed explanations:

Bucket 1 · Fuel savings (0.5–0.6%)

Underinflated tyres burn 0.5–0.6% extra fuel. Most drivers never notice this, because judging tyre pressure by eye is nearly impossible—especially under heavy loads.

Numbers: • 10-wheeler monthly fuel expense: ₹80,000-₹1,20,000 (typical, varies by route) • 0.5–0.6% savings = ₹400-₹720 per month • Annual savings = ₹4,800-₹8,640 Explanation: With Suraksha, you get confirmed correct pressure on every single trip. In the long run, this is the single largest savings contributor. Bucket 2 · Tyre life extension (5-7%)

Tyres wear evenly when maintained at the correct pressure. Uneven wear caused by under- or over-inflation reduces total tyre life by 5-7%.

Numbers: • Set of truck tyres cost: ₹2,00,000-₹3,50,000 • 5-7% extension over tyre lifecycle: ₹10,000-₹24,500 savings per tyre cycle • Typical tyre cycle: 18-24 months for heavy commercial use • Monthly effective savings: ₹500-₹1,500 Bucket 3 · Roadside downtime avoidance

A roadside blowout or major puncture incurs direct costs (towing, repair) plus indirect costs (delivery delays, driver lost time, customer penalties).

Numbers per event: • Towing charges: ₹5,000-₹15,000 • Emergency repair: ₹3,000-₹8,000 • Delivery delay penalty (contract-based): Variable, often ₹5,000-₹25,000 • Driver overtime + waiting cost: ₹1,000-₹3,000 • Total per event: ₹14,000-₹51,000 Frequency: Even 1-2 events per year avoided pays for the entire kit. # [83] Why Suraksha  ·  Safety, Savings, Simplicity for Truck Owner-Drivers | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/suraksha/why-suraksha  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/suraksha/why-suraksha  
**Meta Description:** *Three reasons truck owner-drivers install Suraksha. Safety for the driver. Savings for the business. Simplicity for the family. Full breakdown of each pillar.*  

### Page Content

WHY SURAKSHA?

# SAFETY. SAVINGS. SIMPLICITY.

तीन वजह, क्यों ड्राइवर सुरक्षा लगवाते हैं। ഡ്രൈവർമാർ സുരക്ഷ ഘടിപ്പിക്കുന്നതിന്റെ മൂന്ന് കാരണങ്ങൾ. तीन वजह, क्यों ड्राइवर सुरक्षा लगवाते हैं। ત્રણ કારણ, શા માટે ડ્રાઇવરો સુરક્ષા લગાવે છે. டிரைவர்கள் சுரக்ஷா பொருத்துவதற்கான மூன்று காரணங்கள். డ్రైవర్లు సురక్ష ఎందుకు అమర్చుకుంటారో మూడు కారణాలు. ಡ್ರೈವರ್‌ಗಳು ಸುರಕ್ಷಾ ಅಳವಡಿಸಲು ಮೂರು ಕಾರಣಗಳು. ਤਿੰਨ ਕਾਰਨ, ਡਰਾਈਵਰ ਸੁਰਕਸ਼ਾ ਕਿਉਂ ਲਗਵਾਉਂਦੇ ਹਨ। তিনটি কারণ, কেন ড্রাইভাররা সুরক্ষা লাগান।

Safety, savings, and simplicity. Each pillar is explained in detail below. Read, explore, and decide.

01 · SAFETY Pillar 1: Suraksha Red

## FRONT-TYRE BLOWOUTS, PREVENTED.

Every commercial driver knows what a front-tyre blowout on an Indian highway means. At 60+ km/h, a 40-ton loaded truck pulls violently into the divider or oncoming traffic. In a split second, a driver&#x27;s life, their cargo, and their family&#x27;s sole livelihood are jeopardized.

Suraksha is engineered around human safety, time saved on the road, and protecting driver livelihoods. By continuously streaming real-time tyre pressure and temperature directly to an in-cab display, Suraksha warns drivers 24 to 48 hours before pressure anomalies or thermal stress cause blowouts. You eliminate roadside downtime, prevent dangerous night repairs, and guarantee safe arrival home to your family.

National Highway Safety & Livelihood Statistics:

- • ~1.83–1.9 Lakh annual road fatalities in India: Official national transport data indicates that roughly 13% of highway road fatalities are directly tied to tyre failures, burst tyres, and catastrophic blowouts.

- • Protecting Driver Livelihoods: For an owner-driver, a single catastrophic rollover destroys the truck that feeds the family. Suraksha serves as an indispensable human safety shield and financial safeguard.

- • 24–48 Hour Early Warning: Over 80% of tyre failures begin with invisible thermal spikes or subtle pressure leaks detectable only through precision sensor telemetry.

- • 8–14 Hours Saved Per Route: Eliminates stranded breakdowns on unlit highway shoulders, protecting cargo commitments and keeping drivers safe.

The Suraksha promise: You receive early warning 24 to 48 hours before failure occurs.

SAFETY FOR YOUR FAMILY [Explore Safety In Detail → ](/suraksha/safety) 02 · SAVINGS Pillar 2: Marigold

## NINE MONTHS. FULL PAYBACK.

From a business perspective, Suraksha&#x27;s return on investment is straightforward. Operational savings recover the full cost of the kit within 9 to 12 months.

Where do these savings come from?

Fuel savings (0.5–0.6%): Underinflated tyres consume 0.5–0.6% extra diesel. With Suraksha, tyres stay at optimal operating pressure. For a typical 10-wheeler truck with a monthly fuel bill of ₹80,000–₹1,20,000, a 0.5–0.6% saving delivers ₹400–₹720 back every month.

Tyre life extension (5–7%): Properly inflated tyres last 5–7% longer. With a fresh set of truck tyres costing ₹2,00,000–₹3,50,000, extending tyre life saves thousands across every tyre cycle.

Zero roadside breakdown costs (₹5,000–₹15,000 per event): Every blowout leads to towing charges, vehicle downtime, and missed delivery penalties. Preventing even a single roadside event recovers substantial operational expense.

Nine-month payback calculation:

- • Fuel savings: ₹400–₹720 per month × 9 months = ₹3,600–₹6,480

- • Tyre life extension: Proportional to km driven, typically ₹1,500–₹3,000 per month

- • Downtime avoidance: Prevents costly highway emergency callouts

Total 9-month savings: Typically ₹40,000–₹70,000, easily exceeding initial kit investment.

FULL PAYBACK [Explore Savings In Detail → ](/suraksha/savings) 03 · SIMPLICITY Pillar 3: Ganga Teal

## FIT AT ANY PUNCTURE SHOP. WATCH IN YOUR CABIN.

Above all, Suraksha is simple: no app required, no subscriptions, no monthly fees, and no certified mechanic needed.

Get your kit at any Truck Wheels centre or via WhatsApp. Fit it in 15 minutes at any puncture shop. Monitor tyre pressure and temperature directly on your in-cabin display during every trip.

What you never need to do:

- • No app to download — the in-cabin display is fully standalone

- • No WiFi or mobile data needed — sensors communicate directly via wireless RF

- • No mechanic needed — any roadside puncture shop can complete the fitment

- • No tyre brand restrictions — works seamlessly with any tyre brand

- • No monthly fees — one-time purchase with zero recurring cost

No monthly fees. No app needed.

Real driver experience: Hundreds of trucks install Suraksha every month across transport clusters in India at puncture shops and Truck Wheels centres. Zero training required, zero complications.

SIMPLICITY GUARANTEED [Explore Simplicity In Detail → ](/suraksha/simplicity)

## THREE PROMISES. ONE COMPLETE KIT. COMPLETE SAFETY.

# [84] Technology · Treel Platform Architecture | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/technology  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/technology  
**Meta Description:** *The Treel technology stack: proprietary sensors, edge processing, cloud infrastructure, machine learning, and the Vehicle Digital Twin architecture that powers TMIP.*  

### Page Content

TECHNOLOGY

# The technology behind mobility intelligence.

Treel is built on a five-layer architecture: sensors, edge processing, cloud infrastructure, machine learning, and the operator interface. Each layer is engineered for scale, reliability, and openness.

SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE

## The five-layer stack.

How telemetry flows from physical commercial vehicle wheel-ends through edge gateways, cloud ingestion, and machine learning models to the operator&#x27;s interface:

Layer 05 • Single Source of Truth

### Operator Interface

Enterprise console, driver display, consumer apps & programmatic APIs

The dashboard, the API, the alerts, the reports, the mobile app — all draw from the same Digital Twin data. Enterprise customers access via TMIP. Owner-drivers access via the Suraksha in-cab display. Consumers access via the Personal TPMS mobile app.

TMIP Enterprise Suraksha In-Cab Personal TPMS App REST & Webhooks Layer 04 • Automated Diagnostics

### Machine Learning

Continuous state estimation & predictive maintenance algorithms

Models run continuously against the Vehicle Digital Twin data to forecast component wear, detect anomalies, predict maintenance windows, and identify driver behaviour risks. Model accuracy is tracked, versioned, and validated against real-world outcomes.

Wear Forecasting Anomaly Detection Maintenance Prediction Driver Risk Scoring Layer 03 • Persistent Twin

### Cloud Infrastructure

Persistent Vehicle Digital Twin running on high-availability distributed cloud

The Treel cloud is where the Vehicle Digital Twin lives. Every vehicle has a persistent Twin that receives updates continuously, records state history, and serves queries. Infrastructure is designed for 99.7 percent uptime (current trailing 90-day average) with regional redundancy.

99.7% Trailing Uptime Sub-Second Ingestion Immutable History Regional Failover Layer 02 • Offline Resilient

### Edge Processing

Vehicle-level gateway, signal conditioning & store-and-forward buffering

Edge devices at the vehicle level aggregate sensor data, filter noise, apply first-pass analytics, and stream to the cloud. Edge processing reduces bandwidth cost and enables offline resilience: if connectivity drops, the vehicle continues to log locally.

Store-and-Forward Noise Filtering Dead-Zone Buffering Low-Power Mesh Layer 01 • Proprietary Hardware

### Sensors & Telemetry

Proprietary wheel-end transducers engineered and manufactured in India

Treel sensors are proprietary. ARAI certified. Benchmarked against international TPMS standards. Made in India by our engineering team. Two sensor families: automotive (5-year warranty) and two-wheeler (3-year warranty). The sensor is where the physical vehicle enters the data layer.

ARAI Certified 5-Year Automotive Warranty 3-Year 2W Warranty Made in India

### Page Content

STANDARDS & GOVERNANCE

# Compliance is a floor, not a ceiling.

From automotive manufacturing quality (IATF 16949) to military-grade environmental sealing (IP65 / IP67) and global enterprise data governance (ISO 27001, GDPR), Treel engineering adheres to the strictest tier-1 standards.

### IATF 16949

Automotive Quality Management System aligned for OEM and tier-1 assembly lines.

### ISO 27001

Information Security Management System ensuring end-to-end cloud and telematics data integrity.

### GDPR & DPDP

Comprehensive data privacy frameworks for EU deployments and India DPDP Act 2023.

### FCC & CE

Radio frequency and electromagnetic compatibility certifications for global exports.

AUTOMOTIVE MANUFACTURING STANDARD

## IATF 16949 Automotive Quality Certification

Treel hardware production facilities operate under IATF 16949 standards, the global technical specification and quality management standard for the automotive industry. This ensures zero-defect manufacturing, continuous improvement, and supply chain consistency required by leading commercial vehicle manufacturers.

- PPAP (Production Part Approval Process): Level 3 production qualification for OEM assembly line integration.

- Traceability: Laser-etched individual QR/serial tracking for 100% component-level provenance across sensor lifecycles.

- Automotive Grade Components: AEC-Q qualified semiconductors and silicon pressure cells rated for extreme thermal cycling (-40°C to +125°C).

INGRESS PROTECTION & DURABILITY

## IP65 & IP67 Environmental Sealing

Commercial wheel-ends and off-the-road (OTR) axles face high-pressure jet washing, abrasive slurry, chemical road salts, and full water submersion. Treel external and internal valve-mount sensors meet stringent IP65 and IP67 ingress protection specifications:

#### IP65 Specification

Dust-tight protection against total particulate ingress and resistant to low/medium pressure water jets from all angles during vehicle maintenance washdowns.

#### IP67 Specification

Hermetically sealed against complete dust ingress and continuous operational immersion in water up to 1 metre depth for 30 minutes in water crossings and wet quarry pits.

GLOBAL TELECOMMUNICATIONS & EMC

## FCC & CE Telematics Certifications

To support global fleet export and international cross-border logistics, Treel telemetry hubs and wireless sensor transmissions are certified under North American FCC standards and European CE conformity directives.

- • FCC Part 15 Certified: Validated electromagnetic emissions and radio frequency immunity for commercial vehicle electronics in North America.

- • CE RED (Radio Equipment Directive): Full conformity with European health, electromagnetic compatibility (EMC), and radio spectrum efficiency directives.

- • ARAI & AIS-004 / AIS-140: Indian automotive regulatory compliance for in-vehicle telemetry and sensor safety.

INFORMATION SECURITY MANAGEMENT

## ISO/IEC 27001 Information Security

Treel cloud infrastructure and software development lifecycle are governed by ISO/IEC 27001 controls. We safeguard fleet telemetry, driver analytics, and ERP integration endpoints against unauthorized access, service disruptions, and data tampering.

- • End-to-End Encryption: TLS 1.3 in transit and AES-256 for resting telemetry and Vehicle Digital Twin archives.

- • Role-Based Access Control (RBAC): Granular permissions with mandatory multi-factor authentication (MFA) across all administrative portals.

- • Vulnerability Management: Continuous automated scanning and regular third-party penetration testing.

## India DPDP Act 2023 & European GDPR

Treel is strictly aligned with the India Digital Personal Data Protection Act (DPDP) 2023 and maintains pre-configured readiness for European Union General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) requirements for international fleet operators.

### DPDP Act 2023 Compliance

- • Verifiable consent mechanisms for operator & driver identification

- • Strict purpose limitation tied to contractual fleet management

- • Statutory Data Principal Rights (access, rectification, erasure)

- • Dedicated Data Protection Officer: dpo@treel.in

### GDPR Global Architecture

- • Pre-built Standard Contractual Clauses (SCCs) for cross-border data

- • Automated Data Subject Request (DSR) handling pipelines

- • Pseudonymization of vehicle telemetry and driver behavior records

- • Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIA) on telematics models

## Industry-Specific Safety & Transport Standards

- • Logistics & Long-Haul: State Transport Authority compliant telematics reporting and electronic waybill telemetry integration.

- • Mining & Heavy Haul: Compliance with DGMS (Directorate General of Mines Safety) mandatory safety requirements for off-highway dump trucks and hauliers.

- • Construction & Quarries: Jobsite environmental, vibration, and heavy-equipment operator safety compliance reporting.

- • Ports & Terminal Handling: Severe-duty equipment compliance for reach stackers and straddle carriers operating in high-salinity marine environments.

For Indian enterprise fleets, 100% of Vehicle Digital Twin telemetry and audit data is hosted in MeitY-empaneled tier-4 data centres within India. For international customers, regional data residency clusters in the European Union, MENA, and North America ensure localized compliance with national data sovereignty mandates.

# [89] Predictive Maintenance · TMIP | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/tmip/features/maintenance  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/tmip/features/maintenance  
**Meta Description:** *TMIP&#x27;s predictive maintenance models forecast component failure days or weeks in advance. Reduce unscheduled downtime by 30-40%. Extend maintenance intervals with confidence.*  

### Page Content

PLATFORM · PREDICTIVE MAINTENANCE

# Fix it before it breaks.

TMIP&#x27;s predictive maintenance layer runs machine learning models continuously against every Vehicle Digital Twin. Component wear is scored. Failure windows are forecast. Maintenance is scheduled during planned downtime, not at 3 AM.

MAINTENANCE PLANNING

A component-level view for the workshop, so tyre rotation and replacement are planned, not reactive.

[Book a demo ](/tmip/demo) [View Vehicle Digital Twin ](/vehicle-digital-twin) AT A GLANCE

TMIP&#x27;s predictive maintenance models flag component failures 5-30 days in advance based on longitudinal Vehicle Digital Twin data. Fleet operators reduce unscheduled downtime by 30-40% and extend maintenance intervals safely.

ALGORITHMIC ARCHITECTURE

## How prediction actually happens.

Every Vehicle Digital Twin carries years of state history. Component wear follows patterns — patterns that vary by vehicle class, load, route, climate, and driver. TMIP&#x27;s machine learning models study those patterns across sixty-eight thousand vehicles and apply the learning back to each individual Twin.

Thresholds vs Predictions

Predictions are not thresholds. A tyre reaching 4mm tread depth is a threshold. A tyre with a specific wear signature, on a specific route, at a specific load, at a specific ambient temperature, expected to reach service threshold in 14 days ± 3 days at 85% confidence — that is a prediction.

PREDICTIVE CAPABILITIES

## What TMIP predicts.

The models forecast several categories of vehicle events.

15–45 days in advance

### Tyre replacement windows

15-45 days in advance, ±3-5 day confidence intervals. Predicts remaining tread life and casing fatigue based on route thermal profiles, axle load variance, and real-time pressure histories.

±3–5 day confidence intervals 20–60 days in advance

### Brake component wear

Pad replacement 20-60 days in advance. Forecasts wear rates using cumulative deceleration telemetry, friction temperature accumulation, and duty cycles.

Pad replacement window 30–90 days in advance

### Battery degradation

Failure windows 30-90 days in advance. Identifies internal impedance increases and charging irregularities before starting failures occur on transit routes.

Failure window forecasting Tuned per vehicle

### Engine service events

Oil, coolant, filter windows tuned per vehicle. Computes optimal service intervals based on operating engine hours, load factors, and heat cycles.

Duty cycle calibration Pre-propagation

### Sensor & electrical faults

Anomaly detection catches degrading sensors before misreads propagate across the Vehicle Digital Twin architecture.

Anomaly detection MEASURABLE ROI

## The operating impact.

Fleet operators running TMIP for 12 months see:

30–40%

### Reduction in unscheduled downtime events

Eliminates catastrophic roadside breakdowns and emergency mechanical repairs by identifying degradation well in advance.

Safe Extension

### Extended maintenance intervals

Safely extend maintenance intervals on components running below fleet average wear, maximizing component lifecycle value.

Consolidation

### Consolidated service visits

Cluster multiple predicted maintenance events into single workshop trips, minimizing out-of-service hours for the fleet.

Net ROI

### Reduced total maintenance spend

Achieve lower total maintenance expenditure while maintaining the same or better vehicle health and uptime outcomes.

TRANSPARENT VALIDATION

## How we validate the models.

TMIP tracks every prediction against actual outcomes. Model accuracy is measured per prediction category and published to customers in their operations reports. Underperforming models are retrained on customer-specific data. This is not a black box.

## Eliminate unscheduled breakdowns.

Experience how machine learning models running against vehicle digital twins convert emergency stops into planned depot maintenance.

# [91] Platform Overview  ·  TMIP | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/tmip/platform  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/tmip/platform  
**Meta Description:** *The TMIP platform architecture: five layers from sensor to interface. Vehicle Digital Twin at the core. API-first. Real-time telemetry, predictive maintenance, integrations.*  

### Page Content

PLATFORM

# One architecture. Five layers. Every vehicle a signal.

TMIP is built on a five-layer architecture that turns raw sensor data into operating decisions. This is how the platform works, layer by layer.

## How TMIP is put together.

Every layer of TMIP is engineered independently and integrated cleanly. The sensor layer captures. The edge layer filters. The cloud layer models. The intelligence layer predicts. The interface layer serves. Data flows up; decisions flow down.

### Page Content

PLATFORM · PREDICTIVE MAINTENANCE

# Fix it before it breaks.

TMIP&#x27;s predictive maintenance layer runs machine learning models continuously against every Vehicle Digital Twin. Component wear is scored. Failure windows are forecast. Maintenance is scheduled during planned downtime, not at 3 AM.

[Book a demo ](/tmip/demo) [View Vehicle Digital Twin ](/tmip/platform/vehicle-digital-twin) AT A GLANCE

TMIP&#x27;s predictive maintenance models flag component failures 5–30 days in advance based on longitudinal Vehicle Digital Twin data. Fleet operators reduce unscheduled downtime by 30–40% and extend maintenance intervals safely.

ALGORITHMIC ARCHITECTURE

## How prediction actually happens.

Every Vehicle Digital Twin carries years of state history. Component wear follows patterns — patterns that vary by vehicle class, load, route, climate, and driver. TMIP&#x27;s machine learning models study those patterns across sixty-eight thousand vehicles and apply the learning back to each individual Twin.

Thresholds vs Predictions

Predictions are not thresholds. A tyre reaching 4mm tread depth is a threshold. A tyre with a specific wear signature, on a specific route, at a specific load, at a specific ambient temperature, expected to reach service threshold in 14 days ± 3 days at 85% confidence — that is a prediction.

PREDICTIVE CAPABILITIES

## What TMIP predicts.

The models forecast several categories of vehicle events.

15–45 days in advance

### Tyre replacement windows

Predicts remaining tread life and casing fatigue based on route thermal profiles, axle load variance, and real-time pressure histories.

±3–5 day confidence intervals 20–60 days in advance

### Brake component wear

Forecasts brake pad replacement windows using cumulative deceleration events, friction temperature accumulation, and duty cycles.

Dynamic threshold estimation 30–90 days in advance

### Battery degradation

Identifies early internal resistance increases and charging anomalies before starting failures occur on remote transit corridors.

Cranking voltage trend analysis Tuned per vehicle duty cycle

### Engine service events

Computes optimal oil, coolant, and filter service windows based on engine hours, load factors, and operating temperature profiles.

Dynamic fluid degradation model Early drift detection

### Sensor & electrical faults

Longitudinal anomaly detection catches degrading sensors and intermittent wiring faults before erroneous readings propagate.

Cross-sensor anomaly scoring MEASURABLE ROI

## The operating impact.

Fleet operators running TMIP for 12 months see:

30–40%

### Reduction in unscheduled downtime

Eliminates catastrophic roadside blowouts and emergency mechanical failures by catching degradation early.

Safe Extension

### Extended maintenance intervals

Avoid premature parts replacement on components running below fleet average wear rates, capturing maximum component value.

Multi-Tasking

### Consolidated service visits

Clustering multiple predicted maintenance events into single scheduled depot workshop visits, maximizing vehicle availability.

Net Savings

### Reduced total maintenance spend

Achieves lower total lifecycle cost per kilometre while elevating fleet health benchmarks and customer SLA compliance.

TRANSPARENT VALIDATION

## How we validate the models.

TMIP tracks every prediction against actual outcomes. Model accuracy is measured per prediction category and published to customers in their operations reports. Underperforming models are retrained on customer-specific data. This is not a black box.

## Why the polling frequency changes the operating model.

Legacy fleet software polls every 5–15 minutes. That is fast enough to know where a truck is, but slow enough that you miss the moments that matter: the pressure drop before the puncture, the temperature spike before the brake fade, the driver behaviour pattern before the incident. TMIP samples sub-second, so you see the leading indicators, not just the outcomes.

Legacy 5–15 Min Polling

#### Lagging Indicator Traps

Only reports events after failure has already occurred. You find out about an en-route blowout after the driver pulls over on a highway shoulder.

TMIP Sub-Second Streaming

#### Leading Indicator Protection

Detects micro-pressure loss curves, rapid temperature escalations, and driveline anomalies instantly — triggering automated depot work orders before breakdowns happen.

FLEET MISSION CONTROL

## What operators see.

The TMIP live dashboard shows fleet-wide status, per-vehicle detail, and per-subsystem drill-down. Filter by region, route, driver, vehicle class. Alert thresholds are configurable. Export to CSV or push to your BI stack.

LIVE TELEMETRY STREAM · 68,000+ ASSETS ACTIVE Filter: All Regions Sampling: 1000ms Average Fleet Pressure 118.4 PSI 98.6% in nominal band Wheel-End Temperature 54.2°C Normal highway thermal curve Telemetry Uptime 99.8% Edge buffer active Active Alerts 3 Actionable 0 critical roadside stops ACTIONABLE CONTEXT

TMIP alerts are actionable, not noise. Every alert carries context: what triggered, what the reading is, what the threshold is, what the recommended action is, and who is responsible.

# [94] Vehicle Digital Twin · TMIP | Treel

### Page Content

PLATFORM · VEHICLE DIGITAL TWIN

# Every truck, as a queryable object.

The Vehicle Digital Twin is TMIP&#x27;s architectural core. It turns each vehicle in your fleet into a live software model — one that responds to state changes in real time and answers business questions in structured queries.

[Book a demo ](/tmip/demo) [Read technical whitepaper ](/tmip/whitepapers/vehicle-digital-twin) AT A GLANCE

TMIP&#x27;s Vehicle Digital Twin is a live software representation of a physical truck. State updates continuously from onboard sensors. History is immutable. Predictive models run against the Twin to forecast maintenance, cost, and risk. Fleet operators query the Twin like a database.

DIGITAL TWIN ANATOMY

## What lives inside a Vehicle Digital Twin.

Every TMIP-managed vehicle has three layers of data structured as a persistent Digital Twin.

Real-Time Telemetry

### State layer

Real-time snapshot of every subsystem. Tyre pressures, temperatures, loads. Fuel level, engine parameters, brake wear. Driver behaviour indicators. Location and route context. State updates sub-second as sensors report.

Sub-second frequency 6 subsystems 0–200 PSI range Immutable Time-Series

### History layer

Append-only record of every state change. Timestamped. Immutable. Available for query at any resolution: last hour, last month, last year, the full deployment lifetime. This layer enables anomaly detection, seasonality analysis, and longitudinal ROI attribution.

Full lifecycle retention Time-series index Zero data decay Machine Learning

### Prediction layer

Machine learning models continuously score the Twin. Time-to-failure for critical components. Optimal maintenance windows. Driver risk indices. Route efficiency scores. Predictions carry confidence intervals and are actionable, not theoretical.

15–45 day failure horizons 85%+ confidence intervals Dynamic RUL scoring OPERATIONAL USE CASES

## What Vehicle Digital Twins let you do.

Transforming complex multi-sensor telemetry into structured queries and proactive maintenance decisions.

### Ask real questions.

"Which vehicles in the Bangalore-to-Chennai route have tyre wear >70% and are due for replacement in the next 30 days?"

TMIP returns a structured list with estimated replacement costs, inventory availability, and optimal depot scheduling windows.

### Attribute cost.

True cost per kilometre per vehicle, per route, per driver.

Aggregated by region, class, or contract. Operational cost is attributable to specific maintenance and driving decisions, not averaged across the fleet.

### Predict downtime.

Component wear models flag failure windows 5–30 days in advance.

Fleet operators schedule maintenance during planned depot stops instead of reacting to emergency breakdowns at 3 AM.

### Compare and optimise.

Benchmark vehicles against fleet peers and operating baselines.

Identify outliers in fuel burn and tyre wear. Adjust operational parameters based on empirical data proving what works on specific corridors.

0 1

### Cost per kilometre pressure

The primary efficiency metric measured by enterprise CFOs, heavily degraded by unchecked fuel burn and premature tyre scrapping.

0 2

### Accelerated tyre wear cycles

Long-haul linehaul commercial tyres experience high thermal fatigue and uneven wear patterns, demanding early replacements.

0 3

### Compounding fuel inefficiencies

Driver habits, route elevation variations, and rolling resistance from 10-15 PSI under-inflation silently drain operational margins.

0 4

### Delivery window vulnerabilities

Unscheduled en-route punctures and thermal blowouts directly jeopardize shipper SLA delivery commitments and customer retention.

0 5

### Multi-region visibility gaps

Centralized operations teams in Delhi or Mumbai struggle to detect and remediate remote breakdowns occurring thousands of kilometres away in real time.

## Mining Operating Challenges

Mining operations subject vehicles and instrumentation to the harshest mechanical and environmental stresses in modern industry.

0 1

### Intermittent or absent cellular connectivity

Deep open-cast extraction pits and remote mining leases operate outside cellular tower coverage, causing standard cloud tracking systems to fail completely.

0 2

### Extreme cost of unscheduled downtime

When an ultra-heavy dumper stalls on a single-lane haul ramp, production bottlenecks cascade across the entire pit, costing lakhs per hour in lost output.

0 3

### Aggressive tyre replacement cycles

Massive OTR and heavy commercial tyres wear rapidly under extreme axle payloads, steep haul road gradients, and sharp blasted rock fragments.

0 4

### Remote site maintenance capacity constraints

Mining workshops operate with limited technician availability and high spare parts lead times, making unexpected catastrophic failures catastrophic to production.

0 5

### High-risk, safety-critical environments

Thermal runaway in massive heavy tyres presents severe explosion hazards to personnel, equipment, and structural safety on active mine haul roads.

### Page Content

[Home](/) / [TMIP](/tmip) / Commercial Vehicle TPMS COMMERCIAL VEHICLE TPMS · FLEET & OEM

# Commercial Vehicle TPMS. Engineered for Heavy Fleets.

Heavy commercial vehicle tyre telematics, multi-axle pressure and temperature monitoring, and digital twin telemetry engineered for medium and heavy truck manufacturers and enterprise fleet operators.

[Inquire for Commercial Fleets ](/contact?subject=tmip) [Explore TMIP Enterprise Platform](/tmip) 68,000+ Vehicles Under Management 85%+ Blowout Incident Reduction 5–7% Tyre-Life Extension 9 Months Median Payback Period CAPABILITIES

## Purpose-built for commercial vehicle operations.

From steer tyres to multi-axle trailer tandems, Treel Commercial Vehicle TPMS delivers real-time visibility, thermal warning protection, and actionable maintenance insights.

### Multi-Axle Pressure Monitoring

Continuous high-precision PSI and bar tracking across steer, drive, lift, and multi-axle trailer wheel positions. Identifies under-inflation immediately before it degrades casing integrity or burns excess fuel.

### Tyre Temperature & Blowout Prevention

Direct internal cavity temperature sensing detects early brake drag, wheel hub bearing friction, and thermal buildup well before catastrophic highway blowout or tyre fire occurs.

### Dual-Wheel Differential Alerts

In twin-wheel assemblies, pressure differences cause the larger tyre to carry excess load and scrub prematurely. Real-time differential alerts eliminate uneven tyre wear across dual tyres.

### CAN J1939 Edge Gateway Telemetry

Integrates with the vehicle&#x27;s CAN bus and communicates wirelessly with wheel sensors. Onboard edge storage guarantees zero telemetry loss during cellular dead zones on national highways.

### Predictive Maintenance & Tread Wear

Machine-learning algorithms analyze pressure-temperature curves to project Remaining Useful Life (RUL) and schedule depot rotations before irreversible tread damage sets in.

### True Cost Per Kilometre Tracking

Measures actual tyre performance and wear rates across axle positions, tyre brands, and retread cycles to provide fleet managers with empirical cost-per-kilometre analytics.

ARCHITECTURE

## From wheel sensors to the TMIP mobility platform.

Commercial Vehicle TPMS combines ruggedized vehicle-grade hardware with the cloud intelligence of TMIP to power real-time fleet operations.

01 HARDWARE

### Ruggedized Wheel Sensors

Internal rim-mount and valve-stem sensors engineered for heavy commercial duty cycles. Rated up to 185 PSI / 12.8 bar and 125°C with multi-year battery longevity and severe vibration resistance.

02 TELEMATICS

### Edge Gateway & CAN Bus

In-cabin or chassis-mounted telematics unit connects wirelessly to wheel sensors and interfaces directly with vehicle CAN J1939 networks with offline telemetry caching.

03 PLATFORM

### TMIP Cloud & Digital Twin

Ingests high-frequency telemetry, calculates thermal expansion compensation, and maps every tyre&#x27;s operational state to its live Vehicle Digital Twin.

04 DISPATCH

### Alert Engine & TMS Sync

Dispatches instant alerts for rapid deflation, high temperature spikes, and pressure mismatch to fleet controllers, maintenance teams, and drivers, syncing with TMS and ERP systems.

MULTI-AXLE VERSATILITY

## Supports up to 24 wheel positions per commercial rig.

Whether deploying on a 4x2 distribution truck or a multi-axle bulk cement tanker with articulated trailers, Treel Commercial Vehicle TPMS handles all commercial chassis layouts.

4x2 & 6x2 Haulage 6x4 Heavy Tippers 8x2 & 8x4 Multi-Axle Trucks Tandem & Tri-Axle Trailers Pneumatic Lift Axles Intercity Bus Coaches

### OEM Integration & Fleet Pilots

Treel partners directly with commercial vehicle OEMs for factory-fit pre-delivery integration, and with large enterprise fleets for managed depot retrofits across India.

## What this paper covers

Across two hundred commercial transport enterprises running 68,000+ heavy commercial vehicles, the median payback period for the Treel Mobility Intelligence Platform (TMIP) is nine months. This empirical research paper dissects the unit economics behind that timeline.

Rather than relying on theoretical fuel savings or generic telematics benchmarks, this study isolates the precise operational interventions that move financial levers: tyre pressure maintenance disciplines, early thermal signature detection, vehicle digital twin telemetry synchronization, and automated preventive maintenance dispatch.

The paper breaks down ROI attribution across four distinct fleet profiles: long-haul linehaul logistics, refrigerated cold-chain networks, intercity passenger coaches, and heavy infrastructure tippers.

<a id="page-108-vehicle-digital-twin"></a>

# [108] What is a Vehicle Digital Twin?  ·  Treel | Treel

**URL:** https://treel.in/vehicle-digital-twin  
**Live Mirror:** https://treel-orcin.vercel.app/vehicle-digital-twin  
**Meta Description:** *A Vehicle Digital Twin is a live software model of a physical vehicle, updated in real time from onboard sensors. It turns each vehicle into a queryable object. Definition, architecture, and business value, explained by Treel.*  

### Page Content

CATEGORY DEFINITION

# What is a Vehicle Digital Twin?

A Vehicle Digital Twin is a live software model of a physical vehicle, continuously updated in real time from onboard sensors . Every subsystem — engine, tyres, fuel, driveline, brakes, electrical — is represented as queryable data. Fleet operators reason about the vehicle the way software engineers reason about their data warehouse: with structured queries, alerts, analytics, and predictive models.

AT A GLANCE

A Vehicle Digital Twin is a live software model of a physical vehicle. It receives real-time data from onboard sensors, represents every subsystem as queryable data, and enables predictive analytics on component health, driver behaviour, and operating cost. TMIP is Treel&#x27;s Vehicle Digital Twin platform, operating across 68,000 vehicles in India.

## What a Vehicle Digital Twin actually contains.

A Vehicle Digital Twin holds three layers of information about the physical vehicle it represents.

### The State Layer

Captures what is true about the vehicle right now: tyre pressures, temperatures, fuel level, load, engine parameters, brake wear, driver behaviour signals. This layer updates continuously as sensors report.

### The History Layer

Captures how the state has changed over time. Every fluctuation is recorded, timestamped, and available for query. History enables trend analysis, seasonality detection, and anomaly identification.

### The Prediction Layer

Applies machine learning models to state and history to forecast future events: when a tyre will need replacement, when a component is likely to fail, when a driver is at risk of an incident. Predictions come with confidence intervals and are actionable, not theoretical.

## The three-step model.

01

### Step 1 · Sensors report

Treel sensors placed on tyres and integrated with vehicle systems report telemetry sub-second. The data is edge-processed to reduce bandwidth and cost, then streamed to Treel&#x27;s cloud infrastructure.

02

### Step 2 · The Twin updates

Each vehicle has its own persistent Digital Twin. As telemetry arrives, the Twin&#x27;s state layer updates, the history layer records, and the prediction layer re-runs its models.

03

### Step 3 · Operators query

Fleet operators access the Digital Twin through the TMIP dashboard, or scheduled reports. They ask questions (“which vehicles are due for tyre replacement in the next 30 days?”) and get answers with the vehicle&#x27;s own data as the source.

## What operators do differently.

The Vehicle Digital Twin changes the operating model of a fleet business. Instead of reacting to failures, operators anticipate them. Instead of averaging costs across the fleet, they attribute cost to specific vehicles, routes, and drivers. Instead of standardising maintenance intervals, they tune them per vehicle. The savings compound: our customers see 5-7% tyre life extension, 0.5–0.6% fuel savings, and 30-40% reduction in unscheduled downtime over a 12-month deployment.

5–7% Tyre Life Extension 0.5–0.6% Direct Fuel Savings 30–40% Downtime Reduction

## The Vehicle Digital Twin runs on TMIP.

TMIP is Treel&#x27;s enterprise Vehicle Digital Twin platform. Every enterprise customer&#x27;s fleet is represented as a portfolio of Digital Twins. The dashboard, the alerts, the predictive maintenance, the ROI calculator all draw from these Twins.

[See it in TMIP ](/tmip/platform/vehicle-digital-twin)

WHY TREEL

# Six reasons the mobility intelligence category runs on Treel.

Operating heavy commercial vehicles across thousands of kilometres of highways has historically meant enduring blind spots, anxiety, and midnight distress calls. We founded Treel on a foundational conviction: mobility should never operate in the dark.

Treel exists to deliver continuous 360-degree visibility that turns physical machine risk into deep emotional safety for everyone connected to the journey.

## Complete Enterprise Case Studies Store (6 Studies)

### Case Study 1: 1000-truck-case-study

**Client/Fleet:** Enterprise Commercial Fleet  
**Metric/Result:** Zero roadside blowouts  
**Summary:**   

<h3>What we built together</h3><p>Treel executed a phased 90-day rollout across seven major maintenance hubs: Stage 01 (Days 1–30) retrofitted ARAI-certified valve-stem sensors to all 18 tyre positions per tractor-trailer unit. Stage 02 (Days 31–60) integrated native bi-directional REST webhooks into the client's custom enterprise dispatch ERP. Stage 03 (Days 61–90) activated digital twin predictive degradation models trained on live corridor telemetry.</p><h3>What changed</h3><p>Today, the operator's central control tower monitors 18,000+ active tyre nodes in real time. Yard inspection shifts no longer waste hours checking 1,000 healthy vehicles; instead, workshop dispatch queues are automatically generated, directing mechanics only to assets displaying active pressure or thermal variances.</p><p>By eliminating running on under-inflated tyres and preventing heat-induced blowouts, the fleet achieved a documented 0.5–0.6% fuel savings and extended median casing life by 6.8%, reaching full project capital payback in 8.6 months. </p>

### Case Study 2: intercity-passenger-express

**Client/Fleet:** Enterprise Commercial Fleet  
**Metric/Result:** Zero roadside blowouts  
**Summary:**   

<h3>What changed</h3><p>Across 18 months of monitored highway operations, the coach fleet experienced zero high-speed tyre blowouts. Early warning alerts allowed drivers to execute controlled stops at scheduled highway food plazas for technician tyre changes.</p><p>Standardized tyre pressure compliance across the fleet reduced rolling friction, resulting in a 0.5% reduction in diesel consumption and elevating overall timetable punctuality to 99.6%.</p><h3>Life on TMIP</h3><p>Depot technicians perform automated pre-trip tyre health inspections on tablet terminals prior to every evening departure. Operations controllers in Pune and Bangalore monitor live passenger comfort scores and corridor thermal alerts in real time, proactively coaching drivers on route management.</p>

### Case Study 3: quarry-transit-tippers

**Client/Fleet:** Enterprise Commercial Fleet  
**Metric/Result:** Zero roadside blowouts  
**Summary:**   

<h3>What changed</h3><p>With real-time pressure monitoring, tipper tyre casings were protected from under-inflated heat delamination. Scrap rates dropped by 14%, and average casing retreadability increased from 1.1 cycles to 2.4 cycles per tyre.</p><p>Site project managers synchronized wheel-end inspections with scheduled batching plant maintenance breaks, cutting unexpected site downtime by 31% and achieving capital payback in 7.4 months.</p><h3>Life on TMIP</h3><p>Project site supervisors now monitor live vehicle health on ruggedized mobile tablets at the batching plant gate. Tippers showing pressure loss are automatically flagged for immediate top-up at the site service bay before returning to the quarry run.</p>

### Case Study 4: open-cast-haulage-analytics

**Client/Fleet:** Enterprise Commercial Fleet  
**Metric/Result:** Zero roadside blowouts  
**Summary:**   

<h3>What changed</h3><p>Within 90 days of deployment, the mining operation achieved continuous sub-second visibility into wheel temperatures and pressure dynamics across all shifts.</p><p>Catastrophic tyre blowouts on active haul ramps dropped to zero over the 18-month evaluation period. Mine superintendents received predictive alerts allowing drivers to pull into maintenance lay-bys before casings suffered permanent structural damage.</p><h3>Life on TMIP</h3><p>Today, the central mine dispatch room tracks real-time thermal curves for every active dumper. Shift handover reports highlight vehicles operating near threshold tolerances, allowing workshop crews to perform targeted tyre pressure balancing and wheel rotation during scheduled shift changes.</p>

### Case Study 5: cold-chain-express-transit

**Client/Fleet:** Enterprise Commercial Fleet  
**Metric/Result:** Zero roadside blowouts  
**Summary:**   

<h3>What changed</h3><p>Over 14 months of operation, the cold-chain fleet recorded zero catastrophic tyre failures in transit. Pre-departure automated gate checks detected 142 slow punctures at the depot before vehicles commenced expressway transit.</p><p>Operating at calibrated nominal pressures lowered total fuel expenditure by 0.6%, delivering ₹1.4 Cr in annualized operating cost reductions while maintaining a 99.8% on-time delivery record.</p><h3>Life on TMIP</h3><p>Reefer dispatch controllers now manage vehicle readiness with green-light departure validation on their TMS screens. Fleet managers receive instant exception alerts if a trailer tyre loses more than 2 PSI during transit, directing the vehicle to scheduled service points without interrupting temperature integrity.</p>

### Case Study 6: oem-pre-delivery-intelligence

**Client/Fleet:** Enterprise Commercial Fleet  
**Metric/Result:** Zero roadside blowouts  
**Summary:**   

<h3>What changed</h3><p>Over 2,500 commercial vehicles were rolled out with pre-configured TMIP factory intelligence across a 12-month period. Fleet buyers were able to deploy vehicles into commercial operation immediately upon delivery with zero retrofit downtime.</p><p>The OEM's service engineering group reduced warranty claim disputes by 14.2% by utilizing verifiable historical operating logs (inflation pressure, thermal cycles, and axle load patterns) to resolve warranty inquiries objectively.</p><h3>Life on TMIP</h3><p>Dealership sales teams now provide enterprise buyers with instant QR-code vehicle onboarding at delivery. The OEM's engineering headquarters monitors aggregate reliability benchmarks across different geographies, using real-world operational stress telemetry to guide future chassis and powertrain refinements.</p>
